## 第二十七章：核心模块实现

### 27.1 环境感知模块实现

```python
import psutil
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict
from collections import deque

@dataclass
class CPUState:
    usage_percent: float
    per_cpu_percent: List[float]
    load_average: tuple
    user_time: float
    system_time: float

@dataclass
class MemoryState:
    total: int
    available: int
    used: int
    percent: float
    swap_total: int
    swap_used: int

@dataclass
class EnvironmentState:
    timestamp: float
    cpu: CPUState
    memory: MemoryState
    disk: 'DiskState'
    network: 'NetworkState'
    processes: 'ProcessState'
    logs: 'LogState'
    files: 'FileState'
    agent: 'AgentState'

class PerceptionModule:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.history = deque(maxlen=config.history_size)
        self.log_collector = LogCollector(config)
        self.file_watcher = FileWatcher(config)

    def sense(self) -> EnvironmentState:
        state = EnvironmentState(
            timestamp=time.time(),
            cpu=self._collect_cpu(),
            memory=self._collect_memory(),
            disk=self._collect_disk(),
            network=self._collect_network(),
            processes=self._collect_processes(),
            logs=self.log_collector.collect(),
            files=self.file_watcher.get_changes(),
            agent=self._collect_agent_state()
        )
        self.history.append(state)
        return state

    def _collect_cpu(self) -> CPUState:
        return CPUState(
            usage_percent=psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.1),
            per_cpu_percent=psutil.cpu_percent(percpu=True),
            load_average=os.getloadavg(),
            user_time=psutil.cpu_times().user,
            system_time=psutil.cpu_times().system
        )

    def _collect_memory(self) -> MemoryState:
        mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
        swap = psutil.swap_memory()
        return MemoryState(
            total=mem.total,
            available=mem.available,
            used=mem.used,
            percent=mem.percent / 100.0,
            swap_total=swap.total,
            swap_used=swap.used
        )

    def _collect_disk(self):
        # 实现磁盘状态采集
        pass

    def _collect_network(self):
        # 实现网络状态采集
        pass

    def _collect_processes(self):
        # 实现进程状态采集
        pass

    def _collect_agent_state(self):
        # 实现Agent自身状态采集
        pass

    def is_idle(self) -> bool:
        # 判断系统是否空闲
        if len(self.history) < 10:
            return False
        recent_cpu = [s.cpu.usage_percent for s in list(self.history)[-10:]]
        return np.mean(recent_cpu) < 5.0
```

### 27.2 驱力引擎实现

```python
class DriveEngine:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.safe_drive = SafeDrive(config)
        self.gain_drive = GainDrive(config)
        self.eff_drive = EffDrive(config)
        self.weight_adjuster = WeightAdjuster(config)

    def compute_gaps(self, state: EnvironmentState) -> DriveGaps:
        # 计算各驱力
        safe_reward = self.safe_drive.calculate(state)
        gain_reward = self.gain_drive.calculate(state)
        eff_reward = self.eff_drive.calculate(state)

        # 动态调整权重
        weights = self.weight_adjuster.adjust(state, self.config.base_weights)

        # 计算总奖励和缺口
        total_reward = (
            weights[0] * safe_reward +
            weights[1] * gain_reward +
            weights[2] * eff_reward
        )

        return DriveGaps(
            safe=1.0 - safe_reward,
            gain=1.0 - gain_reward,
            eff=1.0 - eff_reward,
            total=1.0 - total_reward,
            weights=weights
        )

class SafeDrive:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.anomaly_detector = IsolationForestDetector()

    def calculate(self, state: EnvironmentState) -> float:
        # 异常检测
        anomaly_score = self._detect_anomalies(state)

        # 资源风险评估
        resource_risk = self._assess_resource_risk(state)

        # 未知状态评估
        unknown_score = self._assess_unknown(state)

        # 安全事件评估
        security_score = self._assess_security(state)

        # 综合安全驱力
        safe_reward = 1.0 - (
            0.3 * anomaly_score +
            0.3 * resource_risk +
            0.2 * unknown_score +
            0.2 * security_score
        )

        return max(0.0, min(1.0, safe_reward))

    def _detect_anomalies(self, state):
        # 实现异常检测
        pass

    def _assess_resource_risk(self, state):
        # 实现资源风险评估
        pass

    def _assess_unknown(self, state):
        # 实现未知状态评估
        pass

    def _assess_security(self, state):
        # 实现安全事件评估
        pass
```

### 27.3 任务生成模块实现

```python
class TaskGenerator:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.task_pool = TaskPool(config)
        self.evaluator = TaskEvaluator(config)
        self.exploration_rate = config.exploration_rate

    def generate(self, gaps: DriveGaps) -> List[Task]:
        tasks = []

        # 基于缺口生成任务
        if gaps.safe > self.config.safe_threshold:
            tasks.extend(self._generate_safe_tasks(gaps.safe))

        if gaps.gain > self.config.gain_threshold:
            tasks.extend(self._generate_gain_tasks(gaps.gain))

        if gaps.eff > self.config.eff_threshold:
            tasks.extend(self._generate_eff_tasks(gaps.eff))

        # 添加探索任务
        if random.random() < 0.1:
            tasks.extend(self._generate_exploration_tasks())

        # 更新任务池
        self.task_pool.update(tasks)

        return self.task_pool.get_all()

    def select(self, tasks: List[Task]) -> Task:
        if not tasks:
            return self._default_task()

        # 评估所有任务
        scored_tasks = [(task, self.evaluator.evaluate(task)) for task in tasks]
        scored_tasks.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)

        # 探索-利用选择
        if random.random() < (1 - self.exploration_rate):
            return scored_tasks[0][0]  # 利用
        else:
            return self._exploration_select(scored_tasks)  # 探索

    def _generate_safe_tasks(self, gap):
        return [
            Task("risk_prediction", priority=gap),
            Task("anomaly_detection", priority=gap * 0.8),
            Task("state_monitoring", priority=gap * 0.6),
        ]

    def _generate_gain_tasks(self, gap):
        return [
            Task("unknown_exploration", priority=gap),
            Task("feature_testing", priority=gap * 0.7),
            Task("pattern_mining", priority=gap * 0.6),
        ]

    def _generate_eff_tasks(self, gap):
        return [
            Task("model_pruning", priority=gap),
            Task("redundancy_cleanup", priority=gap * 0.8),
            Task("frequency_optimization", priority=gap * 0.6),
        ]

    def _generate_exploration_tasks(self):
        # 随机生成探索性任务
        pass

    def _exploration_select(self, scored_tasks):
        # 按信息价值加权随机选择
        pass

    def _default_task(self):
        # 默认任务：继续监控
        return Task("state_monitoring", priority=0.5)
```

### 27.4 预测模型实现

```python
import torch
import torch.nn as nn

class MultiLevelPredictor:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.low_level = LowLevelModel(config)
        self.mid_level = MidLevelModel(config)
        self.high_level = HighLevelModel(config)
        self.integrator = PredictionIntegrator(config)

    def predict(self, state: EnvironmentState) -> Prediction:
        # 底层预测
        low_pred = self.low_level.predict(state)

        # 中层预测（基于底层）
        mid_pred = self.mid_level.predict(low_pred)

        # 高层预测（基于中层）
        high_pred = self.high_level.predict(mid_pred)

        # 整合预测
        integrated = self.integrator.integrate(low_pred, mid_pred, high_pred)

        return Prediction(
            low=low_pred,
            mid=mid_pred,
            high=high_pred,
            integrated=integrated
        )

    def update(self, error: ErrorSignal):
        # 更新各层模型
        self.low_level.update(error.low)
        self.mid_level.update(error.mid)
        self.high_level.update(error.high)

class LowLevelModel:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.model = self._build_model()
        self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.model.parameters(), lr=0.001)

    def _build_model(self):
        return nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(self.config.state_dim, 64),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Linear(64, 32),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Linear(32, self.config.state_dim)
        )

    def predict(self, state):
        x = torch.tensor(state.vector, dtype=torch.float32)
        with torch.no_grad():
            pred = self.model(x)
        return pred.numpy()

    def update(self, error):
        # 在线更新
        pass
```

### 27.5 误差评估实现

```python
class ErrorEvaluator:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.error_history = RingBuffer(config.error_history_size)

    def evaluate(self, prediction: Prediction, actual: EnvironmentState) -> ErrorSignal:
        # 计算各层误差
        low_error = self._compute_error(prediction.low, actual.vector)
        mid_error = self._compute_error(prediction.mid, actual.vector)
        high_error = self._compute_error(prediction.high, actual.vector)

        # 标准化误差
        low_error_norm = self._normalize(low_error)
        mid_error_norm = self._normalize(mid_error)
        high_error_norm = self._normalize(high_error)

        # 生成反馈
        feedback = self._generate_feedback(low_error_norm)

        return ErrorSignal(
            low=low_error_norm,
            mid=mid_error_norm,
            high=high_error_norm,
            total=np.mean([low_error_norm, mid_error_norm, high_error_norm]),
            feedback=feedback
        )

    def _compute_error(self, prediction, actual):
        return np.mean((prediction - actual) ** 2)

    def _normalize(self, error):
        # 基于历史误差标准化
        if len(self.error_history) < 100:
            return error
        max_error = max(self.error_history)
        return error / max(max_error, 1e-6)

    def _generate_feedback(self, error):
        if error < 0.1:
            return Feedback.POSITIVE
        elif error > 0.5:
            return Feedback.NEGATIVE
        else:
            return Feedback.NEUTRAL
```

### 27.6 模型优化器实现

```python
class DualModeOptimizer:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.micro_optimizer = MicroOptimizer(config)
        self.restructurer = Restructurer(config)
        self.snapshot_manager = SnapshotManager(config)
        self.mode = OptimizationMode.MICRO

    def update(self, error: ErrorSignal):
        if self.mode == OptimizationMode.MICRO:
            self.micro_optimizer.update(error)

            # 检查是否需要切换到重构模式
            if self._should_restructure(error):
                self._enter_restructure_mode()
        else:
            self.restructurer.update(error)

            # 重构完成或失败，回到微修正模式
            if self.restructurer.is_done():
                self._exit_restructure_mode()

    def _should_restructure(self, error):
        # 持续高误差或长期无提升
        recent_errors = self.micro_optimizer.get_recent_errors(100)
        if len(recent_errors) < 100:
            return False

        mean_error = np.mean(recent_errors)
        if mean_error > self.config.restructure_threshold:
            return True

        # 检查是否长期无提升
        if self._plateau_detected(recent_errors):
            return True

        return False

    def _plateau_detected(self, errors):
        # 线性回归检测趋势
        x = np.arange(len(errors))
        slope, _ = np.polyfit(x, errors, 1)
        return abs(slope) < 0.001  # 几乎无变化

    def _enter_restructure_mode(self):
        self.snapshot_manager.save_snapshot()
        self.mode = OptimizationMode.RESTRUCTURE
        self.restructurer.start()

    def _exit_restructure_mode(self):
        if self.restructurer.success:
            self.snapshot_manager.clear_snapshot()
        else:
            self.snapshot_manager.rollback()
        self.mode = OptimizationMode.MICRO
```

### 27.7 记忆模块实现

```python
import sqlite3
import json
from datetime import datetime

class MemoryModule:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.short_term = ShortTermMemory(config)
        self.long_term = LongTermMemory(config)
        self.offline_reviewer = OfflineReviewer(config)
        self.last_review_time = time.time()

    def save_experience(self, state, task, error):
        experience = Experience(
            timestamp=time.time(),
            state=state,
            task=task,
            error=error
        )

        # 保存到短期记忆
        self.short_term.add(experience)

        # 评估价值
        value = self._assess_value(experience)

        # 高价值经验保存到长期记忆
        if value > self.config.memory_threshold:
            self.long_term.store(experience)

    def offline_review(self):
        if not self._should_review():
            return

        self.offline_reviewer.review(self.short_term, self.long_term)
        self.last_review_time = time.time()

    def _should_review(self):
        elapsed = time.time() - self.last_review_time
        return elapsed > self.config.review_interval

    def _assess_value(self, experience):
        # 基于信息增益、预测难度、稀有度评估价值
        pass

class ShortTermMemory:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.buffer = RingBuffer(config.short_term_size)

    def add(self, experience):
        self.buffer.append(experience)

    def sample(self, batch_size):
        return random.sample(list(self.buffer), min(batch_size, len(self.buffer)))

class LongTermMemory:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.db_path = config.memory_db_path
        self._init_db()

    def _init_db(self):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        conn.execute('''
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS experiences (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                timestamp REAL,
                state TEXT,
                task TEXT,
                error REAL,
                value REAL
            )
        ''')
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

    def store(self, experience):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        conn.execute(
            'INSERT INTO experiences (timestamp, state, task, error, value) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
            (experience.timestamp,
             json.dumps(experience.state.to_dict()),
             experience.task.name,
             experience.error.total,
             experience.value)
        )
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

    def retrieve(self, query, k=100):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.execute(
            'SELECT * FROM experiences ORDER BY value DESC LIMIT ?',
            (k,)
        )
        results = cursor.fetchall()
        conn.close()
        return results
```

## 第二十八章：并发与线程设计

### 28.1 线程架构

系统采用多线程架构，不同模块运行在不同线程中：

```python
import threading
import queue

class AgentThreadManager:
    def __init__(self, agent):
        self.agent = agent
        self.threads = {}
        self.queues = {}

    def start(self):
        # 启动感知线程
        self.queues['perception'] = queue.Queue()
        self.threads['perception'] = threading.Thread(
            target=self._perception_loop,
            daemon=True
        )
        self.threads['perception'].start()

        # 启动预测线程
        self.queues['prediction'] = queue.Queue()
        self.threads['prediction'] = threading.Thread(
            target=self._prediction_loop,
            daemon=True
        )
        self.threads['prediction'].start()

        # 启动任务执行线程
        self.queues['execution'] = queue.Queue()
        self.threads['execution'] = threading.Thread(
            target=self._execution_loop,
            daemon=True
        )
        self.threads['execution'].start()

        # 启动优化线程
        self.queues['optimization'] = queue.Queue()
        self.threads['optimization'] = threading.Thread(
            target=self._optimization_loop,
            daemon=True
        )
        self.threads['optimization'].start()

        # 启动离线复盘线程
        self.threads['review'] = threading.Thread(
            target=self._review_loop,
            daemon=True
        )
        self.threads['review'].start()

    def _perception_loop(self):
        while self.agent.running:
            state = self.agent.perception.sense()
            self.queues['prediction'].put(state)
            time.sleep(1.0)  # 每秒感知一次

    def _prediction_loop(self):
        while self.agent.running:
            try:
                state = self.queues['prediction'].get(timeout=1.0)
                prediction = self.agent.prediction_model.predict(state)
                self.queues['execution'].put((state, prediction))
            except queue.Empty:
                continue

    def _execution_loop(self):
        while self.agent.running:
            try:
                state, prediction = self.queues['execution'].get(timeout=1.0)

                # 计算驱力
                gaps = self.agent.drive_engine.compute_gaps(state)

                # 生成并选择任务
                tasks = self.agent.task_generator.generate(gaps)
                task = self.agent.task_generator.select(tasks)

                # 执行任务
                task.execute()

                # 获取新状态
                next_state = self.agent.perception.sense()

                # 计算误差
                error = self.agent.error_evaluator.evaluate(prediction, next_state)

                # 发送给优化线程
                self.queues['optimization'].put(error)

            except queue.Empty:
                continue

    def _optimization_loop(self):
        while self.agent.running:
            try:
                error = self.queues['optimization'].get(timeout=1.0)
                self.agent.optimizer.update(error)
            except queue.Empty:
                continue

    def _review_loop(self):
        while self.agent.running:
            time.sleep(600)  # 每10分钟检查一次
            if self.agent.perception.is_idle():
                self.agent.memory.offline_review()
```

### 28.2 线程安全

```python
import threading

class ThreadSafeState:
    def __init__(self):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._state = None

    def get(self):
        with self._lock:
            return self._state

    def set(self, state):
        with self._lock:
            self._state = state

class ThreadSafeQueue:
    def __init__(self, maxsize=100):
        self._queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=maxsize)
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def put(self, item, block=True, timeout=None):
        with self._lock:
            if self._queue.full():
                self._queue.get()  # 丢弃最旧的消息
            self._queue.put(item, block, timeout)

    def get(self, block=True, timeout=None):
        return self._queue.get(block, timeout)
```

### 29.2 配置加载

```python
import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict

@dataclass
class AgentConfig:
    name: str
    version: str
    max_cpu_percent: float
    max_memory_percent: float
    max_disk_gb: float
    base_weights: tuple
    thresholds: dict
    exploration_rate: float
    max_pool_size: int
    restructure_threshold: float
    max_restructure_iterations: int
    short_term_size: int
    memory_threshold: float
    review_interval: float
    forbidden_paths: List[str]
    max_exploration_failures: int

    @classmethod
    def from_file(cls, path: str):
        with open(path, 'r') as f:
            config = json.load(f)

        return cls(
            name=config['agent']['name'],
            version=config['agent']['version'],
            max_cpu_percent=config['resources']['max_cpu_percent'],
            max_memory_percent=config['resources']['max_memory_percent'],
            max_disk_gb=config['resources']['max_disk_gb'],
            base_weights=(
                config['drives']['base_weights']['safe'],
                config['drives']['base_weights']['gain'],
                config['drives']['base_weights']['eff']
            ),
            thresholds=config['drives']['thresholds'],
            exploration_rate=config['tasks']['exploration_rate'],
            max_pool_size=config['tasks']['max_pool_size'],
            restructure_threshold=config['optimization']['restructure_threshold'],
            max_restructure_iterations=config['optimization']['max_restructure_iterations'],
            short_term_size=config['memory']['short_term_size'],
            memory_threshold=config['memory']['memory_threshold'],
            review_interval=config['memory']['review_interval'],
            forbidden_paths=config['safety']['forbidden_paths'],
            max_exploration_failures=config['safety']['max_exploration_failures']
        )
```

## 第三十章：日志与监控

### 30.1 日志系统

```python
import logging
from datetime import datetime

class AgentLogger:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.logger = logging.getLogger('SelfEvolvingAgent')
        self.logger.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)

        # 文件处理器
        file_handler = logging.FileHandler('data/logs/agent.log')
        file_handler.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)

        # 控制台处理器
        console_handler = logging.StreamHandler()
        console_handler.setLevel(logging.INFO)

        # 格式化
        formatter = logging.Formatter(
            '%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s'
        )
        file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
        console_handler.setFormatter(formatter)

        self.logger.addHandler(file_handler)
        self.logger.addHandler(console_handler)

    def log_state(self, state):
        self.logger.debug(f"State: CPU={state.cpu.usage_percent:.1f}%, "
                         f"Memory={state.memory.percent:.1f}%")

    def log_drive(self, gaps):
        self.logger.info(f"Drive gaps: Safe={gaps.safe:.3f}, "
                        f"Gain={gaps.gain:.3f}, Eff={gaps.eff:.3f}")

    def log_task(self, task):
        self.logger.info(f"Selected task: {task.name} (priority={task.priority:.3f})")

    def log_error(self, error):
        self.logger.debug(f"Prediction error: {error.total:.3f}")

    def log_evolution(self, metrics):
        self.logger.info(f"Evolution metrics: {metrics}")
```

### 30.2 监控接口

```python
class AgentMonitor:
    def __init__(self, agent):
        self.agent = agent

    def get_status(self):
        return {
            'running': self.agent.running,
            'current_task': self.agent.current_task.name if self.agent.current_task else None,
            'drive_gaps': self.agent.drive_engine.last_gaps.to_dict(),
            'prediction_error': self.agent.error_evaluator.last_error.total,
            'memory_usage': self.agent.memory.usage(),
            'uptime': time.time() - self.agent.start_time
        }

    def get_metrics(self):
        return {
            'prediction_accuracy': self.agent.error_evaluator.accuracy_history(),
            'task_distribution': self.agent.task_generator.task_history(),
            'resource_usage': self.agent.perception.resource_history(),
            'evolution_score': self.agent.optimizer.evolution_score()
        }
```

## 第三十一章：部署与运行

### 31.1 安装脚本

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# install.sh

echo "Installing SelfEvolvingAgent..."

# 创建虚拟环境
python3 -m venv venv
source venv/bin/activate

# 安装依赖
pip install -r requirements.txt

# 创建数据目录
mkdir -p data/memory
mkdir -p data/snapshots
mkdir -p data/logs
mkdir -p data/config

# 复制默认配置
if [ ! -f data/config/agent.json ]; then
    cp config/default.json data/config/agent.json
fi

echo "Installation complete!"
echo "Run 'python main.py' to start the agent."
```

### 31.2 入口文件

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# main.py

import sys
import signal
from agent.core.agent import SelfEvolvingAgent
from agent.core.config import AgentConfig

def main():
    # 加载配置
    config = AgentConfig.from_file('data/config/agent.json')

    # 创建Agent
    agent = SelfEvolvingAgent(config)

    # 信号处理
    def signal_handler(sig, frame):
        print("\nShutting down agent...")
        agent.stop()
        sys.exit(0)

    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, signal_handler)
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, signal_handler)

    # 启动Agent
    print(f"Starting {config.name} v{config.version}...")
    agent.start()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### 31.3 系统服务配置

**Linux systemd服务**：

```ini
# /etc/systemd/system/self-evolving-agent.service
[Unit]
Description=Self Evolving Agent
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=simple
User=agent
WorkingDirectory=/opt/self-evolving-agent
ExecStart=/opt/self-evolving-agent/venv/bin/python main.py
Restart=always
RestartSec=10

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
```

**Windows服务**：

```python
import win32serviceutil
import win32service
import win32event

class AgentService(win32serviceutil.ServiceFramework):
    _svc_name_ = "SelfEvolvingAgent"
    _svc_display_name_ = "Self Evolving Agent"

    def __init__(self, args):
        win32serviceutil.ServiceFramework.__init__(self, args)
        self.stop_event = win32event.CreateEvent(None, 0, 0, None)
        self.agent = None

    def SvcStop(self):
        self.ReportServiceStatus(win32service.SERVICE_STOP_PENDING)
        if self.agent:
            self.agent.stop()
        win32event.SetEvent(self.stop_event)

    def SvcDoRun(self):
        config = AgentConfig.from_file('data/config/agent.json')
        self.agent = SelfEvolvingAgent(config)
        self.agent.start()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    win32serviceutil.HandleCommandLine(AgentService)
```

## 第五卷：进化路径与长期演进

---

## 第三十二章：三阶段进化路线图

### 32.1 第一阶段：基础自驱闭环（第1-3个月）

**目标**：建立最基本的环境感知-驱力计算-任务执行-模型修正闭环。

**核心里程碑**：

**M1.1 环境感知基础设施（第1-2周）**
- 完成系统层监控（CPU、内存、磁盘、网络）
- 完成日志层采集（系统日志、应用日志）
- 完成文件层监控（文件变更、目录状态）
- 实现状态向量标准化输出
- 验收标准：能够每秒输出完整的环境状态向量，延迟<100ms

**M1.2 驱力量化引擎（第3-4周）**
- 实现安全驱力计算（异常检测、资源风险评估）
- 实现收益驱力计算（预测误差、信息增量）
- 实现能效驱力计算（CPU、内存、耗时）
- 实现动态权重调整
- 验收标准：三大驱力计算准确，权重调整合理

**M1.3 基础时序预测（第5-6周）**
- 实现底层时序预测模型（ARIMA、指数平滑）
- 实现预测误差计算
- 实现在线参数微修正
- 验收标准：预测误差比随机预测降低50%

**M1.4 基础任务闭环（第7-8周）**
- 实现基于缺口的任务生成
- 实现基础任务执行框架
- 实现预测-实际比较
- 实现参数级模型更新
- 验收标准：系统能够自主运行1小时，持续产生任务并执行

**M1.5 基础记忆系统（第9-10周）**
- 实现短期记忆（循环缓冲区）
- 实现长期记忆（SQLite存储）
- 实现基础离线复盘
- 验收标准：系统重启后能够恢复状态

**M1.6 资源控制（第11-12周）**
- 实现CPU限制
- 实现内存限制
- 实现自适应采样频率
- 验收标准：系统CPU占用<20%，内存占用<20%

**第一阶段验收标准**：
- 系统能够自主运行24小时不崩溃
- 预测误差呈现下降趋势
- 资源使用始终在安全范围内
- 能够生成并执行多种类型的任务

### 32.2 第二阶段：完整自主闭环（第4-6个月）

**目标**：实现完整的自主决策闭环，包括动态任务池、探索-利用平衡、持久化记忆。

**核心里程碑**：

**M2.1 动态任务池（第13-14周）**
- 实现任务动态生成与淘汰
- 实现任务多维度评估
- 实现任务优先级排序
- 实现任务池大小控制
- 验收标准：任务池始终包含多样化任务，无任务堆积

**M2.2 自主打分决策（第15-16周）**
- 实现任务综合得分计算
- 实现70/30探索-利用选择
- 实现动态中断机制
- 验收标准：系统能够平衡稳定收益和持续探索

**M2.3 多层预测模型（第17-18周）**
- 实现中层序列预测（LSTM）
- 实现高层趋势预测（注意力机制）
- 实现预测整合机制
- 实现预测传播与误差回传
- 验收标准：多层预测优于单层预测

**M2.4 双模式优化器（第19-20周）**
- 实现微修正模式（在线梯度下降）
- 实现结构性重构模式
- 实现模式自动切换
- 实现快照与回滚
- 验收标准：重构失败自动回滚，成功则提升性能

**M2.5 探索-利用平衡（第21-22周）**
- 实现自适应探索率
- 实现探索止损机制
- 实现信息价值评估
- 验收标准：探索不导致性能持续下降

**M2.6 完整记忆系统（第23-24周）**
- 实现记忆价值评估
- 实现选择性记忆
- 实现离线批量复盘
- 实现知识整理
- 验收标准：长期记忆只保留高价值样本，离线复盘提升模型性能

**第二阶段验收标准**：
- 系统能够自主运行7天不崩溃
- 任务选择合理，收益稳定
- 模型能够结构性进化
- 探索行为有正向收益

### 32.3 第三阶段：永续进化闭环（第7-12个月）

**目标**：实现真正的永续进化，包括自动快照回滚、全维度进化指标监控、长期知识积累。

**核心里程碑**：

**M3.1 自动快照回滚（第25-28周）**
- 实现智能快照策略
- 实现自动性能评估
- 实现一键回滚
- 实现回滚后学习
- 验收标准：重构失败100%自动回滚，无人工干预

**M3.2 进化指标监控（第29-32周）**
- 实现预测精度趋势监控
- 实现认知广度增长监控
- 实现能效改进监控
- 实现综合进化评分
- 验收标准：能够准确评估系统进化状态

**M3.3 长期知识积累（第33-36周）**
- 实现知识提取
- 实现知识表示
- 实现知识迁移
- 实现知识推理
- 验收标准：学到的知识能够迁移到新场景

**M3.4 高级探索策略（第37-40周）**
- 实现基于好奇心的探索
- 实现基于信息增益的探索
- 实现基于不确定性的探索
- 实现组合探索策略
- 验收标准：探索效率比随机探索高3倍

**M3.5 自适应架构（第41-44周）**
- 实现模型结构自适应
- 实现任务类型自适应
- 实现采样频率自适应
- 实现资源分配自适应
- 验收标准：系统能够根据环境自动调整架构

**M3.6 群体学习（第45-52周）**
- 实现多Agent通信（可选）
- 实现经验共享
- 实现知识聚合
- 实现分布式学习
- 验收标准：多Agent学习优于单Agent

**第三阶段验收标准**：
- 系统能够自主运行30天不崩溃
- 进化指标持续上升
- 知识库持续积累
- 能够适应环境重大变化

## 第三十四章：测试策略

### 34.1 单元测试

每个模块都需要编写单元测试：

```python
import pytest
from agent.modules.perception import PerceptionModule
from agent.core.config import AgentConfig

class TestPerceptionModule:
    def setup_method(self):
        self.config = AgentConfig.from_file('tests/test_config.json')
        self.module = PerceptionModule(self.config)

    def test_sense(self):
        state = self.module.sense()
        assert state is not None
        assert state.cpu is not None
        assert state.memory is not None
        assert state.timestamp > 0

    def test_cpu_collection(self):
        cpu = self.module._collect_cpu()
        assert 0 <= cpu.usage_percent <= 100
        assert len(cpu.per_cpu_percent) > 0

    def test_memory_collection(self):
        memory = self.module._collect_memory()
        assert memory.total > 0
        assert 0 <= memory.percent <= 1
```

### 34.2 集成测试

测试模块间的交互：

```python
class TestIntegration:
    def test_perception_to_drive(self):
        agent = SelfEvolvingAgent(self.config)
        state = agent.perception.sense()
        gaps = agent.drive_engine.compute_gaps(state)
        assert gaps.safe >= 0
        assert gaps.gain >= 0
        assert gaps.eff >= 0

    def test_task_execution(self):
        agent = SelfEvolvingAgent(self.config)
        gaps = DriveGaps(safe=0.5, gain=0.3, eff=0.2)
        tasks = agent.task_generator.generate(gaps)
        assert len(tasks) > 0
        task = agent.task_generator.select(tasks)
        assert task is not None
```

### 34.3 性能测试

测试系统性能：

```python
class TestPerformance:
    def test_prediction_latency(self):
        agent = SelfEvolvingAgent(self.config)
        state = agent.perception.sense()

        start = time.time()
        prediction = agent.prediction_model.predict(state)
        latency = time.time() - start

        assert latency < 0.1  # 预测延迟<100ms

    def test_resource_usage(self):
        agent = SelfEvolvingAgent(self.config)
        agent.start()
        time.sleep(60)

        cpu_usage = agent.perception.get_agent_cpu()
        memory_usage = agent.perception.get_agent_memory()

        assert cpu_usage < 20
        assert memory_usage < 20
```

### 34.4 稳定性测试

测试系统长期运行稳定性：

```python
class TestStability:
    def test_24h_run(self):
        agent = SelfEvolvingAgent(self.config)
        agent.start()

        errors = []
        for _ in range(24 * 60):  # 24小时，每分钟检查
            time.sleep(60)
            errors.append(agent.error_evaluator.last_error.total)
            assert agent.running

        # 误差应该呈下降趋势
        first_half = np.mean(errors[:len(errors)//2])
        second_half = np.mean(errors[len(errors)//2:])
        assert second_half < first_half
```

## 第三十五章：风险评估与应对

### 35.1 技术风险

**风险1：预测模型不收敛**
- 概率：中
- 影响：高
- 应对：
  - 使用多种模型并行运行
  - 实现自动学习率调整
  - 实现模型回滚机制
  - 设置预测误差上限

**风险2：探索导致性能下降**
- 概率：中
- 影响：中
- 应对：
  - 实现探索止损机制
  - 限制探索比例
  - 实现快速回滚
  - 设置探索收益下限

**风险3：资源使用超限**
- 概率：低
- 影响：高
- 应对：
  - 严格的资源监控
  - 自适应频率调整
  - 紧急清理机制
  - 硬限制（操作系统级别）

**风险4：数据存储膨胀**
- 概率：中
- 影响：中
- 应对：
  - 自动清理机制
  - 存储上限
  - 数据压缩
  - 分层存储

### 35.2 设计风险

**风险1：驱力设计不合理**
- 概率：中
- 影响：高
- 应对：
  - 大量实验验证
  - 参数可调
  - 多种预设配置
  - 人工干预接口

**风险2：任务生成不全面**
- 概率：中
- 影响：中
- 应对：
  - 丰富的任务模板
  - 动态任务生成
  - 任务组合机制
  - 人工添加任务接口

**风险3：进化停滞**
- 概率：低
- 影响：高
- 应对：
  - 强制探索机制
  - 定期重构
  - 外部扰动注入
  - 进化指标监控

### 35.3 运营风险

**风险1：系统崩溃**
- 概率：低
- 影响：高
- 应对：
  - 异常捕获
  - 自动重启
  - 状态恢复
  - 日志记录

**风险2：安全漏洞**
- 概率：低
- 影响：高
- 应对：
  - 路径白名单
  - 权限限制
  - 行为审计
  - 人工审查

## 第三十七章：总结与展望

### 37.1 核心贡献

本白皮书提出了一种全新的AI系统范式——自驱永续进化预测修正Agent。这种范式的核心贡献包括：

1. **无外部任务驱动**：系统不依赖人工任务指令，完全自主运行。
2. **原生驱力自驱**：内置仿生驱力，自主产生动机和行为。
3. **永续进化能力**：无限目标+持续探索，确保系统永不停滞。
4. **双闭环运行**：在线实时修正+离线深度复盘。
5. **安全可控进化**：刚性边界约束，确保系统安全。

### 37.2 关键创新点

1. **缺口驱动机制**：将动机建模为状态差距，实现真正的自主驱动。
2. **动态任务池**：任务不是预定义的，而是根据缺口动态生成。
3. **探索-利用平衡**：固定比例确保稳定收益和持续进化。
4. **双模式优化**：微修正+结构性重构，兼顾稳定和跃迁。
5. **记忆价值评估**：选择性记忆，只保留高价值信息。

### 37.3 未来展望

自驱永续进化Agent代表了AI系统的一个重要发展方向。未来的研究可以在以下方向深入：

1. **更复杂的驱力体系**：引入更多类型的驱力，如社交驱力、创造驱力等。
2. **更高级的预测模型**：引入世界模型、因果模型等。
3. **更广泛的环境交互**：不仅感知，还能主动影响环境。
4. **更丰富的任务类型**：从监控扩展到控制、优化、创造。
5. **更强大的知识系统**：从记忆到理解、推理、创造。

### 37.4 结语

自驱永续进化预测修正Agent系统是一个雄心勃勃的项目，它试图复刻人类大脑最核心的运行机制——持续预测、实时修正、自主驱动、永续进化。

这个系统的价值不仅在于其技术实现，更在于其背后的哲学：真正的智能不是被动执行任务，而是主动探索世界、主动学习知识、主动提升能力。

我们相信，随着技术的进步和对大脑理解的深入，这种自主认知进化的AI系统将成为未来智能的重要形态，为人类社会带来巨大的价值。

## 第三十八章：预测编码深度解析

### 38.1 预测编码的数学基础

预测编码理论建立在变分贝叶斯推断的数学框架之上。理解这个框架对于深入理解系统的工作原理至关重要。

**贝叶斯推断基础**：

在贝叶斯框架中，我们关注后验概率分布：

$$P(x|y) = \frac{P(y|x)P(x)}{P(y)}$$

其中：
- $P(x|y)$ 是后验概率（给定观测$y$时状态$x$的概率）
- $P(y|x)$ 是似然（给定状态$x$时观测$y$的概率）
- $P(x)$ 是先验概率（状态$x$的初始概率）
- $P(y)$ 是证据（观测$y$的总概率）

**变分推断**：

直接计算后验分布通常是不可行的，因此我们使用变分推断来近似。我们引入一个变分分布$q(x)$来近似真实的后验$P(x|y)$。

两个分布之间的差异用KL散度来衡量：

$$D_{KL}[q(x)||P(x|y)] = \int q(x) \log\frac{q(x)}{P(x|y)} dx$$

**自由能的推导**：

通过数学变换，我们可以将KL散度重写为：

$$D_{KL}[q(x)||P(x|y)] = \underbrace{\int q(x) \log\frac{q(x)}{P(y,x)} dx}_{\text{自由能 } F} + \log P(y)$$

由于$\log P(y)$是常数，最小化KL散度等价于最小化自由能$F$。

**自由能的分解**：

自由能可以进一步分解为：

$$F = \underbrace{-\mathbb{E}_q[\log P(y|x)]}_{\text{期望能量}} + \underbrace{D_{KL}[q(x)||P(x)]}_{\text{复杂度}}$$

- 期望能量：衡量模型预测与观测的匹配程度
- 复杂度：衡量变分分布与先验的差异（正则化项）

### 38.2 层级预测编码

在层级预测编码中，每一层都有自己的生成模型和变分分布。

**层级生成模型**：

$$P(x^{(1)}, x^{(2)}, ..., x^{(L)}, y) = P(y|x^{(1)}) \prod_{l=1}^{L-1} P(x^{(l)}|x^{(l+1)}) P(x^{(L)})$$

**层级变分分布**：

$$q(x^{(1)}, x^{(2)}, ..., x^{(L)}) = \prod_{l=1}^{L} q(x^{(l)}|x^{(l+1)})$$

**层级自由能**：

$$F = \sum_{l=1}^{L} F^{(l)}$$

其中每一层的自由能为：

$$F^{(l)} = -\mathbb{E}_q[\log P(x^{(l)}|x^{(l+1)})] + D_{KL}[q(x^{(l)})||P(x^{(l)})]$$

**预测误差的角色**：

定义预测误差为：

$$\varepsilon^{(l)} = x^{(l)} - \mu^{(l)}$$

其中$\mu^{(l)}$是第$l$层对下一层的预测。

在拉普拉斯近似下（假设分布是高斯的），自由能可以近似为：

$$F \approx \sum_{l=1}^{L} \frac{1}{2\sigma_l^2} \|\varepsilon^{(l)}\|^2 + \text{常数}$$

这就是预测编码中误差最小化的数学基础。

### 38.3 预测编码的实现细节

**前向传播（预测）**：

```python
def forward_pass(higher_level_prediction, generative_model):
    """
    将高层预测向下传播到低层
    """
    # 使用生成模型将高层表示转换为低层预测
    lower_level_prediction = generative_model(higher_level_prediction)
    return lower_level_prediction
```

**反向传播（误差）**：

```python
def backward_pass(actual, prediction, error_model):
    """
    计算预测误差并向上传播
    """
    # 计算局部误差
    local_error = actual - prediction

    # 计算误差对高层表示的梯度
    gradient_to_higher = error_model.gradient(local_error)

    return local_error, gradient_to_higher
```

**参数更新**：

```python
def update_parameters(model, error, learning_rate):
    """
    基于误差更新生成模型的参数
    """
    # 计算梯度
    gradients = compute_gradients(model, error)

    # 更新参数
    for param, grad in zip(model.parameters(), gradients):
        param -= learning_rate * grad
```

### 38.4 预测编码的优势

**计算效率**：

预测编码只需要处理预测误差，而不是完整的感官输入。这意味着：
- 当预测准确时，神经活动最小化
- 只有当出现意外时才需要大量计算
- 符合大脑的能量效率原则

**自然的学习信号**：

预测误差本身就是学习的信号：
- 误差大 → 模型不准确 → 需要学习
- 误差小 → 模型准确 → 不需要学习
- 不需要人工设计损失函数

**层级抽象**：

不同层级学习不同抽象程度的表示：
- 低层：具体特征（边缘、颜色）
- 中层：对象部分（形状、纹理）
- 高层：抽象概念（物体、场景）

**主动推断**：

预测可以影响感知本身：
- 高层预测向下传播，影响低层处理
- 这解释了注意力和预期效应
- 系统可以主动寻找信息来验证预测

### 38.5 预测编码的局限性

**计算复杂度**：

虽然预测编码在概念上优雅，但实现起来计算复杂度较高：
- 需要维护多层表示
- 需要同时优化状态和参数
- 层级间的通信开销

**收敛问题**：

预测编码的优化可能遇到收敛问题：
- 局部最优
- 梯度消失/爆炸
- 层级间的优化冲突

**生物学合理性**：

虽然预测编码受到神经科学的启发，但具体的实现细节与大脑的实际机制仍有差距：
- 真实的神经动力学更复杂
- 存在多种类型的神经元和连接
- 神经调质的影响

---

## 第三十九章：驱力系统的数学建模

### 39.1 驱力的动力学模型

驱力可以被建模为一个动态系统，其状态随时间演化。

**驱力状态方程**：

$$\frac{dD}{dt} = \alpha(G - D) - \beta A$$

其中：
- $D$：当前驱力水平
- $G$：目标缺口（驱动驱力的来源）
- $A$：已采取的行动（减小驱力）
- $\alpha$：缺口响应速率
- $\beta$：行动效果系数

**稳态分析**：

在稳态下，$\frac{dD}{dt} = 0$：

$$D^* = G - \frac{\beta}{\alpha}A$$

这意味着：
- 如果没有行动（$A=0$），驱力等于缺口
- 行动可以减小驱力
- 当行动完全消除缺口时，驱力为零

**多驱力耦合**：

多个驱力之间存在耦合：

$$\frac{dD_i}{dt} = \alpha_i(G_i - D_i) - \beta_i A_i - \sum_{j \neq i} \gamma_{ij} D_j$$

其中$\gamma_{ij}$表示驱力$j$对驱力$i$的抑制或促进作用。

### 39.2 缺口计算的具体方法

**安全缺口计算**：

安全缺口基于风险评估：

$$G_{safe} = \sum_{k} w_k \cdot R_k$$

其中$R_k$是第$k$类风险，$w_k$是其权重。

风险类型包括：
- 系统异常风险：$R_{anomaly} = 1 - P(normal)$
- 资源耗尽风险：$R_{resource} = \max(0, \frac{usage - threshold}{1 - threshold})$
- 未知状态风险：$R_{unknown} = \frac{\text{unknown_count}}{\text{total_count}}$
- 安全事件风险：$R_{security} = \min(1, \frac{\text{event_count}}{\text{threshold}})$

**收益缺口计算**：

收益缺口基于认知状态：

$$G_{gain} = 1 - (w_{accuracy} \cdot A + w_{coverage} \cdot C + w_{novelty} \cdot N)$$

其中：
- $A$：预测准确度
- $C$：认知覆盖度
- $N$：新颖信息获取率

**能效缺口计算**：

能效缺口基于资源使用：

$$G_{eff} = \sum_{k} w_k \cdot \frac{consumption_k - ideal_k}{max_k - ideal_k}$$

### 39.3 权重调整的优化问题

权重调整可以形式化为一个优化问题。

**目标函数**：

$$\max_{W} \mathbb{E}[R_{total}] - \lambda \cdot \text{Var}(R_{total})$$

其中：
- $W = (W_{safe}, W_{gain}, W_{eff})$：权重向量
- $R_{total} = W \cdot R$：总奖励
- $\lambda$：风险厌恶系数

**约束条件**：

$$W_{safe} + W_{gain} + W_{eff} = 1$$
$$W_i \geq 0, \quad \forall i$$

**场景特定的权重**：

不同场景下的最优权重可以通过学习获得：

$$W^*(s) = \arg\max_W \mathbb{E}[R_{total} | S=s]$$

其中$s$是场景特征向量。

### 39.4 驱力与行为的映射

**行为选择概率**：

行为的选择可以用softmax函数建模：

$$P(a|D) = \frac{\exp(Q(a, D)/\tau)}{\sum_{a'} \exp(Q(a', D)/\tau)}$$

其中：
- $Q(a, D)$：在驱力$D$下执行行为$a$的价值
- $\tau$：温度参数（控制探索程度）

**行为价值函数**：

$$Q(a, D) = \mathbb{E}[R_{immediate} + \gamma R_{future} | a, D]$$

**驱力-行为学习**：

通过经验学习驱力和行为之间的关系：

$$\Delta Q(a, D) = \alpha \cdot \delta \cdot \frac{\partial Q}{\partial \theta}$$

其中$\delta = R + \gamma \max_{a'} Q(a', D') - Q(a, D)$是时序差分误差。

---

## 第四十章：任务系统的完整设计

### 40.1 任务的形式化定义

**任务元组**：

一个任务可以形式化为一个六元组：

$$T = (ID, Type, Params, Pre, Post, Constraints)$$

其中：
- $ID$：任务唯一标识
- $Type$：任务类型（安全/收益/能效）
- $Params$：任务参数
- $Pre$：前置条件
- $Post$：后置效果
- $Constraints$：约束条件（时间、资源等）

**任务状态机**：

任务在其生命周期中经历以下状态：

```
CREATED → QUEUED → RUNNING → COMPLETED
   ↓         ↓          ↓
CANCELLED  FAILED    INTERRUPTED
```

**任务依赖图**：

任务之间可能存在依赖关系：

$$T_i \prec T_j \iff Post(T_i) \cap Pre(T_j) \neq \emptyset$$

### 40.2 任务生成算法详解

**基于模板的生成**：

```python
class TaskTemplate:
    def __init__(self, task_type, base_params, precondition):
        self.task_type = task_type
        self.base_params = base_params
        self.precondition = precondition

    def instantiate(self, context):
        if not self.precondition(context):
            return None

        params = self.fill_params(context)
        return Task(
            type=self.task_type,
            params=params,
            priority=self.calculate_priority(context)
        )

    def fill_params(self, context):
        # 根据上下文填充参数
        params = {}
        for key, template in self.base_params.items():
            params[key] = template.format(**context)
        return params
```

**基于规划的生成**：

```python
class TaskPlanner:
    def __init__(self, domain_model):
        self.domain = domain_model

    def plan(self, initial_state, goal_state):
        # 使用A*搜索找到任务序列
        open_set = PriorityQueue()
        open_set.put((0, initial_state, []))

        while not open_set.empty():
            cost, state, plan = open_set.get()

            if self.is_goal(state, goal_state):
                return plan

            for action in self.domain.get_actions(state):
                new_state = self.domain.apply(action, state)
                new_cost = cost + self.cost(action)
                new_plan = plan + [action]

                open_set.put((new_cost + self.heuristic(new_state), new_state, new_plan))

        return None
```

**基于学习的生成**：

```python
class LearnedTaskGenerator:
    def __init__(self, model):
        self.model = model  # 神经网络或决策树

    def generate(self, state, gaps):
        # 将状态和缺口编码为特征向量
        features = self.encode(state, gaps)

        # 使用模型预测任务参数
        task_params = self.model.predict(features)

        # 解码为任务
        task = self.decode(task_params)

        return task
```

### 40.3 任务评估的完整框架

**多目标评估**：

任务评估需要考虑多个目标：

$$Score(T) = \sum_i w_i \cdot f_i(T)$$

评估维度包括：

1. **预期收益**：
   $$f_{benefit}(T) = \mathbb{E}[\Delta G | T]$$

2. **执行成本**：
   $$f_{cost}(T) = \sum_{r} c_r \cdot u_r(T)$$
   其中$c_r$是资源$r$的单位成本，$u_r(T)$是任务$T$对资源$r$的使用量。

3. **成功概率**：
   $$f_{success}(T) = P(success | T, context)$$

4. **时间紧迫性**：
   $$f_{urgency}(T) = 1 - \exp(-\lambda \cdot t_{remaining})$$

5. **信息价值**：
   $$f_{info}(T) = I(T; \text{future})$$

**不确定性处理**：

对于不确定的评估，使用置信区间：

$$Score(T) \in [\hat{S}(T) - \sigma(T), \hat{S}(T) + \sigma(T)]$$

在任务选择时，可以使用乐观估计（UCB策略）：

$$Score_{UCB}(T) = \hat{S}(T) + c \cdot \sigma(T)$$

### 40.4 任务调度算法

**优先级调度**：

```python
def priority_schedule(tasks, resources):
    # 按优先级排序
    sorted_tasks = sorted(tasks, key=lambda t: t.priority, reverse=True)

    schedule = []
    resource_usage = {r: 0 for r in resources}

    for task in sorted_tasks:
        # 检查资源可用性
        if can_allocate(task, resource_usage, resources):
            allocate(task, resource_usage)
            schedule.append(task)

    return schedule
```

**约束满足调度**：

```python
class ConstraintScheduler:
    def __init__(self, constraints):
        self.constraints = constraints

    def schedule(self, tasks):
        # 构建约束图
        graph = self.build_constraint_graph(tasks)

        # 使用回溯搜索找到满足所有约束的调度
        return self.backtrack_search(graph)

    def backtrack_search(self, graph, assignment=None):
        if assignment is None:
            assignment = {}

        if len(assignment) == len(graph.nodes):
            return assignment

        # 选择下一个要调度的任务
        task = self.select_unassigned(graph, assignment)

        for time_slot in self.get_possible_slots(task, assignment):
            if self.is_consistent(task, time_slot, assignment):
                assignment[task] = time_slot

                result = self.backtrack_search(graph, assignment)
                if result is not None:
                    return result

                del assignment[task]

        return None
```

**在线调度**：

```python
class OnlineScheduler:
    def __init__(self, policy):
        self.policy = policy
        self.current_schedule = []

    def on_new_task(self, task):
        # 新任务到达时重新调度
        self.current_schedule = self.policy.reschedule(
            self.current_schedule + [task]
        )

    def on_task_complete(self, task):
        # 任务完成时更新调度
        self.current_schedule.remove(task)
        self.current_schedule = self.policy.optimize(
            self.current_schedule
        )

    def on_interrupt(self, high_priority_task):
        # 高优先级任务中断当前执行
        self.preempt_current()
        self.current_schedule.insert(0, high_priority_task)
```

## 第四十一章：记忆系统的深度设计

### 41.1 记忆的层级结构

**感觉记忆（Sensory Memory）**：

- 持续时间：毫秒到秒级
- 容量：几乎无限
- 内容：原始感官数据的短暂保持
- 实现：循环神经网络的隐藏状态

**短期记忆（Short-Term Memory）**：

- 持续时间：秒到分钟级
- 容量：有限（7±2个组块）
- 内容：当前任务相关的信息
- 实现：循环缓冲区

**工作记忆（Working Memory）**：

- 持续时间：分钟级
- 容量：有限
- 内容：正在处理的信息和操作
- 实现：注意力机制

**长期记忆（Long-Term Memory）**：

- 持续时间：小时到永久
- 容量：几乎无限
- 内容：知识和经验
- 实现：数据库存储

### 41.2 记忆的编码与检索

**编码过程**：

```python
class MemoryEncoder:
    def __init__(self, feature_extractor, compressor):
        self.feature_extractor = feature_extractor
        self.compressor = compressor

    def encode(self, experience):
        # 提取特征
        features = self.feature_extractor(experience)

        # 压缩表示
        compressed = self.compressor.compress(features)

        # 添加元数据
        memory_item = MemoryItem(
            content=compressed,
            timestamp=experience.timestamp,
            context=experience.context,
            emotional_tag=experience.emotional_tag
        )

        return memory_item
```

**检索过程**：

```python
class MemoryRetriever:
    def __init__(self, index, similarity_metric):
        self.index = index
        self.similarity = similarity_metric

    def retrieve(self, query, k=10):
        # 编码查询
        query_vector = self.encode_query(query)

        # 在索引中搜索
        candidates = self.index.search(query_vector, k*10)

        # 精确相似度计算
        similarities = [(item, self.similarity(query_vector, item.vector))
                       for item in candidates]

        # 排序并返回前k个
        similarities.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
        return [item for item, _ in similarities[:k]]

    def retrieve_by_context(self, context, k=10):
        # 基于上下文过滤
        all_memories = self.index.get_all()
        filtered = [m for m in all_memories if self.context_match(m.context, context)]

        # 按时间排序
        filtered.sort(key=lambda m: m.timestamp, reverse=True)
        return filtered[:k]
```

### 41.3 记忆的巩固机制

**系统巩固**：

```python
class SystemConsolidation:
    def __init__(self, hippocampus, cortex):
        self.hippocampus = hippocampus  # 短期记忆
        self.cortex = cortex  # 长期记忆

    def consolidate(self, sleep_phase='slow_wave'):
        # 从海马体重放记忆
        replay_buffer = self.hippocampus.get_recent_experiences()

        for experience in replay_buffer:
            # 在皮层中重新激活相关模式
            cortical_pattern = self.cortex.activate(experience)

            # 强化相关连接
            self.cortex.strengthen_connections(cortical_pattern)

            # 将记忆转移到皮层
            if self.should_transfer(experience):
                self.cortex.store(experience)
                self.hippocampus.mark_for_deletion(experience)

    def should_transfer(self, experience):
        # 基于重复次数和重要性决定是否转移
        replay_count = experience.replay_count
        importance = experience.importance_score

        return replay_count > 3 or importance > 0.8
```

**突触巩固**：

```python
class SynapticConsolidation:
    def __init__(self, model):
        self.model = model

    def consolidate(self, experiences):
        for experience in experiences:
            # 计算预测误差
            prediction = self.model.predict(experience.state)
            error = experience.outcome - prediction

            # 计算梯度
            gradients = self.model.compute_gradients(error)

            # 应用标签依赖的巩固（标签效应）
            consolidation_rate = self.get_consolidation_rate(experience)

            # 更新权重
            for param, grad in zip(self.model.parameters(), gradients):
                param += consolidation_rate * grad

    def get_consolidation_rate(self, experience):
        # 基于情感标签和重复次数确定巩固率
        base_rate = 0.001
        emotional_boost = 1.0 + experience.emotional_intensity
        repetition_boost = 1.0 + 0.1 * experience.replay_count

        return base_rate * emotional_boost * repetition_boost
```

### 41.4 记忆的遗忘机制

**被动遗忘**：

```python
class PassiveForgetting:
    def __init__(self, decay_rate):
        self.decay_rate = decay_rate

    def decay(self, memory_trace, time_elapsed):
        # 指数衰减
        strength = memory_trace.strength * exp(-self.decay_rate * time_elapsed)
        memory_trace.strength = strength

        # 如果强度低于阈值，遗忘
        if strength < self.threshold:
            return True  # 应该遗忘
        return False
```

**主动遗忘**：

```python
class ActiveForgetting:
    def __init__(self, memory_store):
        self.memory = memory_store

    def interfere(self, new_memory):
        # 检索可能受干扰的旧记忆
        similar_memories = self.memory.retrieve_similar(new_memory, threshold=0.8)

        for old_memory in similar_memories:
            # 如果新记忆与旧记忆冲突，削弱旧记忆
            if self.is_conflicting(new_memory, old_memory):
                self.weaken(old_memory)

    def weaken(self, memory):
        memory.strength *= 0.9

        if memory.strength < self.threshold:
            self.memory.delete(memory)
```

**动机性遗忘**：

```python
class MotivatedForgetting:
    def __init__(self, emotional_regulator):
        self.emotional_regulator = emotional_regulator

    def should_forget(self, memory):
        # 如果记忆引起负面情绪且不重要，遗忘
        if (memory.emotional_tag == 'negative' and
            memory.importance < 0.3):
            return True

        # 如果记忆与当前目标冲突，遗忘
        if self.conflicts_with_goals(memory):
            return True

        return False
```

## 第四十二章：进化算法的深度优化

### 42.1 进化策略（Evolution Strategies）

**基本算法**：

```python
class EvolutionStrategy:
    def __init__(self, population_size, sigma, learning_rate):
        self.population_size = population_size
        self.sigma = sigma
        self.lr = learning_rate
        self.theta = None  # 当前参数

    def initialize(self, param_shape):
        self.theta = np.random.randn(*param_shape)

    def evolve(self, fitness_function, generations):
        for gen in range(generations):
            # 生成扰动种群
            epsilon = np.random.randn(self.population_size, *self.theta.shape)
            population = self.theta + self.sigma * epsilon

            # 评估适应度
            fitness = [fitness_function(ind) for ind in population]

            # 计算加权平均
            weights = self.compute_weights(fitness)
            gradient = np.sum(weights[:, None, None] * epsilon, axis=0)

            # 更新参数
            self.theta += self.lr * gradient

            print(f"Generation {gen}: Best fitness = {max(fitness)}")

        return self.theta

    def compute_weights(self, fitness):
        # 使用适应度排名计算权重
        ranks = np.argsort(np.argsort(fitness))
        weights = np.maximum(0, np.log(self.population_size / 2 + 1) - np.log(ranks + 1))
        return weights / np.sum(weights)
```

**协方差矩阵自适应（CMA-ES）**：

```python
class CMAES:
    def __init__(self, dim, population_size=None):
        self.dim = dim
        self.population_size = population_size or 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))

        # 初始化
        self.mean = np.zeros(dim)
        self.sigma = 1.0
        self.C = np.eye(dim)  # 协方差矩阵
        self.pc = np.zeros(dim)  # 进化路径
        self.ps = np.zeros(dim)  # 共轭进化路径

        # 参数
        self.cc = 4 / (dim + 4)
        self.cs = (self.mu_eff + 2) / (dim + self.mu_eff + 5)
        self.c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3)**2 + self.mu_eff)
        self.cmu = min(1 - self.c1, 2 * (self.mu_eff - 2 + 1/self.mu_eff) / ((dim + 2)**2 + self.mu_eff))

    def sample(self):
        # 从多元正态分布采样
        samples = []
        for _ in range(self.population_size):
            z = np.random.randn(self.dim)
            y = self.mean + self.sigma * (self.C @ z)
            samples.append(y)
        return samples

    def update(self, samples, fitnesses):
        # 按适应度排序
        sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitnesses)[::-1]
        sorted_samples = [samples[i] for i in sorted_indices]

        # 更新均值
        old_mean = self.mean.copy()
        self.mean = np.mean(sorted_samples[:self.mu], axis=0)

        # 更新进化路径
        self.ps = (1 - self.cs) * self.ps + np.sqrt(self.cs * (2 - self.cs) * self.mu_eff) * (self.mean - old_mean) / self.sigma

        # 更新协方差矩阵
        self.pc = (1 - self.cc) * self.pc + np.sqrt(self.cc * (2 - self.cc) * self.mu_eff) * (self.mean - old_mean) / self.sigma

        # C更新...
```

### 42.2 遗传算法

```python
class GeneticAlgorithm:
    def __init__(self, population_size, crossover_rate, mutation_rate):
        self.population_size = population_size
        self.crossover_rate = crossover_rate
        self.mutation_rate = mutation_rate

    def initialize_population(self, genome_length):
        return [self.random_genome(genome_length) for _ in range(self.population_size)]

    def evolve(self, fitness_func, generations):
        population = self.initialize_population()

        for gen in range(generations):
            # 评估适应度
            fitness = [fitness_func(ind) for ind in population]

            # 选择
            parents = self.selection(population, fitness)

            # 交叉
            offspring = self.crossover(parents)

            # 变异
            offspring = self.mutate(offspring)

            # 替换
            population = self.replacement(population, offspring, fitness)

        return max(population, key=fitness_func)

    def selection(self, population, fitness):
        # 锦标赛选择
        parents = []
        for _ in range(len(population)):
            contestants = np.random.choice(len(population), 3, replace=False)
            winner = max(contestants, key=lambda i: fitness[i])
            parents.append(population[winner])
        return parents

    def crossover(self, parents):
        offspring = []
        for i in range(0, len(parents), 2):
            if i + 1 < len(parents) and np.random.random() < self.crossover_rate:
                child1, child2 = self.single_point_crossover(parents[i], parents[i+1])
                offspring.extend([child1, child2])
            else:
                offspring.extend([parents[i], parents[i+1] if i+1 < len(parents) else parents[i]])
        return offspring

    def mutate(self, offspring):
        for i in range(len(offspring)):
            if np.random.random() < self.mutation_rate:
                offspring[i] = self.bit_flip_mutation(offspring[i])
        return offspring
```

### 42.3 神经进化

```python
class NEAT:
    def __init__(self, input_size, output_size, population_size):
        self.input_size = input_size
        self.output_size = output_size
        self.population_size = population_size

        self.genome_id = 0
        self.innovation_number = 0
        self.species = []

    def create_initial_population(self):
        population = []
        for _ in range(self.population_size):
            genome = self.create_minimal_genome()
            population.append(genome)
        return population

    def create_minimal_genome(self):
        # 创建最小网络（无隐藏层）
        genome = Genome(self.genome_id)
        self.genome_id += 1

        # 添加输入节点
        for i in range(self.input_size):
            genome.add_node(NodeGene(i, 'input'))

        # 添加输出节点
        for i in range(self.output_size):
            genome.add_node(NodeGene(self.input_size + i, 'output'))

        # 添加连接
        for i in range(self.input_size):
            for j in range(self.output_size):
                connection = ConnectionGene(i, self.input_size + j,
                                          weight=np.random.randn(),
                                          innovation=self.innovation_number)
                self.innovation_number += 1
                genome.add_connection(connection)

        return genome

    def mutate_genome(self, genome):
        # 结构变异
        if np.random.random() < 0.03:
            genome = self.add_node_mutation(genome)
        if np.random.random() < 0.05:
            genome = self.add_connection_mutation(genome)

        # 权重变异
        for connection in genome.connections.values():
            if np.random.random() < 0.8:
                if np.random.random() < 0.9:
                    connection.weight += np.random.randn() * 0.1
                else:
                    connection.weight = np.random.randn()

        return genome

    def speciate(self, population):
        # 将种群划分为物种
        for genome in population:
            found_species = False
            for species in self.species:
                if self.compatibility_distance(genome, species.representative) < 3.0:
                    species.add(genome)
                    found_species = True
                    break

            if not found_species:
                new_species = Species(genome)
                self.species.append(new_species)
```

## 第四十三章：安全与伦理框架

### 43.1 安全约束的形式化

**资源约束**：

```python
class ResourceConstraint:
    def __init__(self, max_cpu, max_memory, max_disk):
        self.max_cpu = max_cpu
        self.max_memory = max_memory
        self.max_disk = max_disk

    def check(self, action):
        estimated_cpu = action.estimate_cpu()
        estimated_memory = action.estimate_memory()
        estimated_disk = action.estimate_disk()

        violations = []
        if estimated_cpu > self.max_cpu:
            violations.append(f"CPU: {estimated_cpu}% > {self.max_cpu}%")
        if estimated_memory > self.max_memory:
            violations.append(f"Memory: {estimated_memory}% > {self.max_memory}%")
        if estimated_disk > self.max_disk:
            violations.append(f"Disk: {estimated_disk}GB > {self.max_disk}GB")

        return len(violations) == 0, violations

    def enforce(self, agent):
        # 实时监控
        while agent.running:
            cpu = agent.get_cpu_usage()
            memory = agent.get_memory_usage()

            if cpu > self.max_cpu:
                agent.throttle(cpu - self.max_cpu)

            if memory > self.max_memory:
                agent.cleanup_memory()

            time.sleep(1)
```

**行为约束**：

```python
class BehaviorConstraint:
    def __init__(self, forbidden_paths, forbidden_actions):
        self.forbidden_paths = forbidden_paths
        self.forbidden_actions = forbidden_actions

    def validate(self, action):
        # 检查动作类型
        if action.type in self.forbidden_actions:
            return False, f"Action type {action.type} is forbidden"

        # 检查路径
        if hasattr(action, 'path'):
            for forbidden in self.forbidden_paths:
                if action.path.startswith(forbidden):
                    return False, f"Path {action.path} is forbidden"

        # 检查参数
        if hasattr(action, 'params'):
            for key, value in action.params.items():
                if self.is_dangerous(key, value):
                    return False, f"Parameter {key}={value} is dangerous"

        return True, None

    def is_dangerous(self, key, value):
        dangerous_keywords = ['rm -rf', 'format', 'delete', 'drop']
        value_str = str(value).lower()
        return any(keyword in value_str for keyword in dangerous_keywords)
```

### 43.2 伦理决策框架

**功利主义框架**：

```python
class UtilitarianEthics:
    def evaluate(self, action, stakeholders):
        total_utility = 0

        for stakeholder in stakeholders:
            utility = self.calculate_utility(action, stakeholder)
            total_utility += utility

        return total_utility

    def calculate_utility(self, action, stakeholder):
        # 考虑各种因素
        factors = {
            'wellbeing': self.impact_on_wellbeing(action, stakeholder),
            'autonomy': self.impact_on_autonomy(action, stakeholder),
            'privacy': self.impact_on_privacy(action, stakeholder),
            'fairness': self.impact_on_fairness(action, stakeholder)
        }

        # 加权求和
        weights = {'wellbeing': 0.4, 'autonomy': 0.3, 'privacy': 0.2, 'fairness': 0.1}
        utility = sum(factors[k] * weights[k] for k in factors)

        return utility
```

**义务论框架**：

```python
class DeontologicalEthics:
    def __init__(self):
        self.rules = [
            Rule("Do not harm", priority=1),
            Rule("Respect autonomy", priority=2),
            Rule("Be truthful", priority=3),
            Rule("Keep promises", priority=4)
        ]

    def evaluate(self, action):
        violations = []

        for rule in self.rules:
            if not rule.check(action):
                violations.append(rule)

        # 按优先级排序
        violations.sort(key=lambda r: r.priority)

        return len(violations) == 0, violations

    def get_permission(self, action, rule):
        # 检查是否有更高优先级的规则允许违反此规则
        for higher_rule in self.rules:
            if higher_rule.priority < rule.priority:
                if higher_rule.permits_violation(action, rule):
                    return True
        return False
```

**美德伦理框架**：

```python
class VirtueEthics:
    def __init__(self):
        self.virtues = {
            'wisdom': 0.5,
            'courage': 0.5,
            'temperance': 0.5,
            'justice': 0.5
        }

    def evaluate(self, action):
        # 评估行动如何影响美德的培养
        virtue_impacts = {}

        for virtue in self.virtues:
            impact = self.assess_virtue_impact(action, virtue)
            virtue_impacts[virtue] = impact

        # 综合评估
        score = sum(virtue_impacts[v] * self.virtues[v] for v in self.virtues)

        return score

    def update_virtues(self, action, outcome):
        # 根据行动结果更新美德权重
        for virtue in self.virtues:
            if self.virtue_promoted(action, virtue, outcome):
                self.virtues[virtue] = min(1.0, self.virtues[virtue] + 0.01)
            elif self.virtue_hindered(action, virtue, outcome):
                self.virtues[virtue] = max(0.0, self.virtues[virtue] - 0.01)
```

## 第四十四章：性能优化技术

### 44.1 计算优化

**向量化计算**：

```python
import numpy as np

# 非向量化（慢）
def compute_slow(data):
    result = []
    for i in range(len(data)):
        result.append(data[i] ** 2 + data[i] * 2)
    return result

# 向量化（快）
def compute_fast(data):
    return data ** 2 + data * 2

# 使用Numba加速
from numba import jit

@jit(nopython=True)
def compute_numba(data):
    result = np.empty_like(data)
    for i in range(len(data)):
        result[i] = data[i] ** 2 + data[i] * 2
    return result
```

**并行计算**：

```python
from multiprocessing import Pool
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

# 多进程并行
def parallel_map(func, data, n_workers=4):
    with Pool(n_workers) as pool:
        results = pool.map(func, data)
    return results

# 多线程并行（适用于IO密集型）
def parallel_thread_map(func, data, n_workers=4):
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=n_workers) as executor:
        results = list(executor.map(func, data))
    return results

# 异步执行
import asyncio

async def async_execute(tasks):
    results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
    return results
```

**缓存优化**：

```python
from functools import lru_cache

@lru_cache(maxsize=1000)
def expensive_computation(x):
    # 耗时的计算
    result = 0
    for i in range(1000000):
        result += x * i
    return result

# 自定义缓存
class ComputationCache:
    def __init__(self, max_size=1000):
        self.cache = {}
        self.max_size = max_size
        self.access_count = {}

    def get(self, key):
        if key in self.cache:
            self.access_count[key] += 1
            return self.cache[key]
        return None

    def put(self, key, value):
        if len(self.cache) >= self.max_size:
            # LRU淘汰
            lru_key = min(self.access_count, key=self.access_count.get)
            del self.cache[lru_key]
            del self.access_count[lru_key]

        self.cache[key] = value
        self.access_count[key] = 1
```

### 44.2 内存优化

**内存池**：

```python
class MemoryPool:
    def __init__(self, object_size, pool_size):
        self.object_size = object_size
        self.pool = [np.zeros(object_size) for _ in range(pool_size)]
        self.available = list(range(pool_size))

    def allocate(self):
        if not self.available:
            raise MemoryError("Pool exhausted")
        idx = self.available.pop()
        return self.pool[idx], idx

    def deallocate(self, idx):
        self.available.append(idx)
        self.pool[idx].fill(0)  # 清零
```

**数据压缩**：

```python
import zlib
import pickle

class CompressedStorage:
    def store(self, data):
        serialized = pickle.dumps(data)
        compressed = zlib.compress(serialized, level=6)
        return compressed

    def retrieve(self, compressed_data):
        decompressed = zlib.decompress(compressed_data)
        data = pickle.loads(decompressed)
        return data

# 稀疏矩阵存储
from scipy import sparse

def store_sparse(matrix):
    sparse_matrix = sparse.csr_matrix(matrix)
    return sparse_matrix
```

**内存映射**：

```python
import mmap
import os

class MemoryMappedFile:
    def __init__(self, filepath, size):
        self.filepath = filepath
        self.size = size

        # 创建文件
        with open(filepath, 'wb') as f:
            f.write(b'\x00' * size)

        # 内存映射
        self.file = open(filepath, 'r+b')
        self.mmap = mmap.mmap(self.file.fileno(), size)

    def write(self, offset, data):
        self.mmap[offset:offset+len(data)] = data

    def read(self, offset, length):
        return self.mmap[offset:offset+length]

    def close(self):
        self.mmap.close()
        self.file.close()
```

### 44.3 存储优化

**分层存储**：

```python
class TieredStorage:
    def __init__(self):
        self.l1_cache = {}  # 内存
        self.l2_cache = {}  # 本地SSD
        self.l3_storage = {}  # 远程存储

    def get(self, key):
        # L1缓存
        if key in self.l1_cache:
            return self.l1_cache[key]

        # L2缓存
        if key in self.l2_cache:
            value = self.l2_cache[key]
            self.l1_cache[key] = value  # 提升到L1
            return value

        # L3存储
        if key in self.l3_storage:
            value = self.l3_storage[key]
            self.l2_cache[key] = value
            self.l1_cache[key] = value
            return value

        return None

    def put(self, key, value, tier='l1'):
        if tier == 'l1':
            self.l1_cache[key] = value
        elif tier == 'l2':
            self.l2_cache[key] = value
        elif tier == 'l3':
            self.l3_storage[key] = value
```

**数据分区**：

```python
class PartitionedStorage:
    def __init__(self, n_partitions):
        self.n_partitions = n_partitions
        self.partitions = [[] for _ in range(n_partitions)]

    def get_partition(self, key):
        return hash(key) % self.n_partitions

    def insert(self, key, value):
        partition_id = self.get_partition(key)
        self.partitions[partition_id].append((key, value))

    def query(self, key):
        partition_id = self.get_partition(key)
        partition = self.partitions[partition_id]

        for k, v in partition:
            if k == key:
                return v
        return None
```

## 第四十五章：监控与诊断系统

### 45.1 指标收集

**系统指标**：

```python
class SystemMetricsCollector:
    def __init__(self):
        self.metrics = {}
        self.history = RingBuffer(10000)

    def collect(self):
        metrics = {
            'cpu_usage': psutil.cpu_percent(),
            'memory_usage': psutil.virtual_memory().percent,
            'disk_usage': psutil.disk_usage('/').percent,
            'network_io': psutil.net_io_counters(),
            'process_count': len(psutil.pids())
        }

        self.metrics = metrics
        self.history.append((time.time(), metrics))

        return metrics

    def get_trend(self, metric_name, window=100):
        values = [m[metric_name] for _, m in list(self.history)[-window:]]
        return {
            'mean': np.mean(values),
            'std': np.std(values),
            'min': np.min(values),
            'max': np.max(values),
            'trend': self.calculate_trend(values)
        }
```

**业务指标**：

```python
class BusinessMetricsCollector:
    def __init__(self, agent):
        self.agent = agent
        self.task_metrics = defaultdict(list)
        self.prediction_metrics = []

    def record_task(self, task, outcome):
        self.task_metrics[task.type].append({
            'duration': outcome.duration,
            'success': outcome.success,
            'reward': outcome.reward,
            'timestamp': time.time()
        })

    def record_prediction(self, prediction, actual):
        error = np.mean((prediction - actual) ** 2)
        self.prediction_metrics.append({
            'error': error,
            'timestamp': time.time()
        })

    def get_summary(self):
        return {
            'task_success_rate': self.calculate_success_rate(),
            'avg_prediction_error': np.mean([m['error'] for m in self.prediction_metrics[-100:]]),
            'tasks_per_minute': self.calculate_throughput()
        }
```

### 45.2 异常检测

**统计异常检测**：

```python
class StatisticalAnomalyDetector:
    def __init__(self, window_size=100):
        self.window_size = window_size
        self.history = RingBuffer(window_size)

    def update(self, value):
        self.history.append(value)

    def is_anomaly(self, value, threshold=3.0):
        if len(self.history) < 30:
            return False

        mean = np.mean(self.history)
        std = np.std(self.history)

        if std == 0:
            return False

        z_score = abs(value - mean) / std
        return z_score > threshold
```

**机器学习异常检测**：

```python
class MLAnomalyDetector:
    def __init__(self, model):
        self.model = model
        self.threshold = 0.95

    def fit(self, X):
        self.model.fit(X)
        scores = self.model.decision_function(X)
        self.threshold = np.percentile(scores, 95)

    def predict(self, x):
        score = self.model.decision_function([x])[0]
        return score < self.threshold, score
```

```python
import json
import logging

class StructuredLogger:
    def __init__(self, name):
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(name)

    def log_event(self, event_type, **kwargs):
        log_entry = {
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'event_type': event_type,
            'data': kwargs
        }
        self.logger.info(json.dumps(log_entry))

    def log_error(self, error, context=None):
        log_entry = {
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'event_type': 'error',
            'error_type': type(error).__name__,
            'error_message': str(error),
            'context': context
        }
        self.logger.error(json.dumps(log_entry))
```

**日志聚合**：

```python
class LogAggregator:
    def __init__(self):
        self.events = []

    def add(self, log_entry):
        self.events.append(log_entry)

    def aggregate_by_type(self, time_window=3600):
        cutoff = time.time() - time_window
        recent_events = [e for e in self.events if e['timestamp'] > cutoff]

        aggregation = defaultdict(lambda: {'count': 0, 'errors': 0})
        for event in recent_events:
            event_type = event['event_type']
            aggregation[event_type]['count'] += 1
            if event_type == 'error':
                aggregation[event_type]['errors'] += 1

        return aggregation

    def find_patterns(self):
        # 使用序列模式挖掘
        sequences = self.extract_sequences()
        patterns = self.mine_patterns(sequences)
        return patterns
```

**自动修复建议**：

```python
class AutoRemediator:
    def __init__(self, knowledge_base):
        self.kb = knowledge_base

    def diagnose_and_remediate(self, problem):
        # 诊断问题
        diagnosis = self.diagnose(problem)

        # 查找修复方案
        solutions = self.kb.find_solutions(diagnosis)

        # 评估每个方案
        scored_solutions = []
        for solution in solutions:
            score = self.evaluate_solution(solution, problem)
            scored_solutions.append((solution, score))

        # 选择最佳方案
        scored_solutions.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
        best_solution = scored_solutions[0][0]

        # 执行修复
        return self.execute_remediation(best_solution)

    def evaluate_solution(self, solution, problem):
        # 评估方案的成功率、成本、风险等
        success_rate = solution.historical_success_rate
        cost = solution.estimated_cost
        risk = solution.risk_level

        return success_rate / (cost * (1 + risk))
```

```python
class EnvironmentConfig:
    def __init__(self):
        self.env = os.getenv('AGENT_ENV', 'development')
        self.config = self.load_config()

    def load_config(self):
        base_config = self.load_file('config/base.json')
        env_config = self.load_file(f'config/{self.env}.json')

        # 合并配置
        merged = self.merge_configs(base_config, env_config)

        # 应用环境变量覆盖
        merged = self.apply_env_overrides(merged)

        return merged

    def apply_env_overrides(self, config):
        # 从环境变量读取覆盖
        for key, value in os.environ.items():
            if key.startswith('AGENT_'):
                path = key[6:].lower().split('_')
                self.set_nested_value(config, path, value)

        return config
```

### 46.3 备份与恢复

**自动备份**：

```python
class BackupManager:
    def __init__(self, backup_dir, retention_days=30):
        self.backup_dir = backup_dir
        self.retention_days = retention_days

    def create_backup(self):
        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
        backup_path = os.path.join(self.backup_dir, f'backup_{timestamp}')

        os.makedirs(backup_path, exist_ok=True)

        # 备份数据库
        self.backup_database(backup_path)

        # 备份配置文件
        self.backup_configs(backup_path)

        # 备份模型快照
        self.backup_snapshots(backup_path)

        # 创建备份清单
        self.create_manifest(backup_path)

        return backup_path

    def backup_database(self, backup_path):
        import shutil
        db_path = 'data/memory/agent.db'
        if os.path.exists(db_path):
            shutil.copy2(db_path, os.path.join(backup_path, 'agent.db'))

    def cleanup_old_backups(self):
        cutoff = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=self.retention_days)

        for backup_name in os.listdir(self.backup_dir):
            backup_path = os.path.join(self.backup_dir, backup_name)
            if os.path.isdir(backup_path):
                # 解析备份时间
                try:
                    backup_time = datetime.strptime(backup_name, 'backup_%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
                    if backup_time < cutoff:
                        shutil.rmtree(backup_path)
                except ValueError:
                    pass
```

**恢复流程**：

```python
class RecoveryManager:
    def __init__(self, backup_manager):
        self.backup_manager = backup_manager

    def list_backups(self):
        backups = []
        for name in os.listdir(self.backup_manager.backup_dir):
            if name.startswith('backup_'):
                backups.append(name)
        return sorted(backups, reverse=True)

    def restore(self, backup_name):
        backup_path = os.path.join(self.backup_manager.backup_dir, backup_name)

        if not os.path.exists(backup_path):
            raise ValueError(f"Backup {backup_name} not found")

        # 验证备份完整性
        if not self.verify_backup(backup_path):
            raise ValueError("Backup is corrupted")

        # 停止Agent
        self.stop_agent()

        # 恢复数据库
        self.restore_database(backup_path)

        # 恢复配置
        self.restore_configs(backup_path)

        # 恢复快照
        self.restore_snapshots(backup_path)

        # 重启Agent
        self.start_agent()

    def verify_backup(self, backup_path):
        manifest_path = os.path.join(backup_path, 'manifest.json')
        if not os.path.exists(manifest_path):
            return False

        with open(manifest_path) as f:
            manifest = json.load(f)

        # 验证所有文件存在且校验和正确
        for file_info in manifest['files']:
            file_path = os.path.join(backup_path, file_info['path'])
            if not os.path.exists(file_path):
                return False

            # 验证校验和
            actual_checksum = self.compute_checksum(file_path)
            if actual_checksum != file_info['checksum']:
                return False

        return True
```
