## 第四十七章：扩展与集成

### 47.1 插件系统

**插件接口**：

```python
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

class AgentPlugin(ABC):
    @abstractmethod
    def initialize(self, agent):
        """初始化插件"""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def on_state_change(self, old_state, new_state):
        """状态变化时调用"""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def on_task_complete(self, task, outcome):
        """任务完成时调用"""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def shutdown(self):
        """关闭插件"""
        pass

class PluginManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.plugins = []

    def load_plugin(self, plugin_class, config=None):
        plugin = plugin_class()
        plugin.initialize(self.agent, config)
        self.plugins.append(plugin)

    def notify_state_change(self, old_state, new_state):
        for plugin in self.plugins:
            try:
                plugin.on_state_change(old_state, new_state)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Plugin {plugin.name} error: {e}")

    def shutdown_all(self):
        for plugin in self.plugins:
            try:
                plugin.shutdown()
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Plugin {plugin.name} shutdown error: {e}")
```

### 47.2 API集成

**REST API**：

```python
from flask import Flask, jsonify, request

class AgentAPI:
    def __init__(self, agent):
        self.agent = agent
        self.app = Flask(__name__)
        self.setup_routes()

    def setup_routes(self):
        @self.app.route('/health', methods=['GET'])
        def health():
            return jsonify({'status': 'healthy', 'running': self.agent.running})

        @self.app.route('/status', methods=['GET'])
        def status():
            return jsonify({
                'current_task': self.agent.current_task.to_dict() if self.agent.current_task else None,
                'drive_gaps': self.agent.drive_engine.last_gaps.to_dict(),
                'uptime': time.time() - self.agent.start_time
            })

        @self.app.route('/metrics', methods=['GET'])
        def metrics():
            return jsonify(self.agent.monitor.get_metrics())

        @self.app.route('/tasks', methods=['POST'])
        def create_task():
            data = request.json
            task = Task(data['type'], data.get('params', {}))
            self.agent.task_generator.add_manual_task(task)
            return jsonify({'task_id': task.id, 'status': 'queued'})

        @self.app.route('/config', methods=['GET', 'PUT'])
        def config():
            if request.method == 'GET':
                return jsonify(self.agent.config.to_dict())
            else:
                new_config = request.json
                self.agent.update_config(new_config)
                return jsonify({'status': 'updated'})

    def run(self, host='0.0.0.0', port=8080):
        self.app.run(host=host, port=port, threaded=True)
```

**WebSocket实时通信**：

```python
import asyncio
import websockets
import json

class AgentWebSocket:
    def __init__(self, agent):
        self.agent = agent
        self.clients = set()

    async def register(self, websocket):
        self.clients.add(websocket)

    async def unregister(self, websocket):
        self.clients.remove(websocket)

    async def broadcast(self, message):
        if self.clients:
            await asyncio.gather(
                *[client.send(json.dumps(message)) for client in self.clients]
            )

    async def handler(self, websocket, path):
        await self.register(websocket)
        try:
            async for message in websocket:
                data = json.loads(message)
                response = await self.handle_message(data)
                await websocket.send(json.dumps(response))
        finally:
            await self.unregister(websocket)

    async def handle_message(self, data):
        command = data.get('command')
        if command == 'get_status':
            return {'status': self.agent.get_status()}
        elif command == 'trigger_task':
            task_type = data.get('task_type')
            self.agent.trigger_manual_task(task_type)
            return {'status': 'triggered'}
        else:
            return {'error': 'Unknown command'}

    async def start_notification_loop(self):
        while True:
            if self.agent.running:
                await self.broadcast({
                    'type': 'status_update',
                    'data': self.agent.get_status()
                })
            await asyncio.sleep(5)
```

### 47.3 外部系统集成

**Prometheus监控**：

```python
from prometheus_client import Counter, Histogram, Gauge, start_http_server

class PrometheusExporter:
    def __init__(self, agent):
        self.agent = agent

        # 定义指标
        self.prediction_error = Gauge('agent_prediction_error', 'Current prediction error')
        self.task_count = Counter('agent_tasks_total', 'Total tasks executed', ['type'])
        self.task_duration = Histogram('agent_task_duration_seconds', 'Task execution duration')
        self.drive_gap = Gauge('agent_drive_gap', 'Current drive gap', ['drive_type'])
        self.memory_usage = Gauge('agent_memory_bytes', 'Agent memory usage')

    def update_metrics(self):
        # 更新指标
        self.prediction_error.set(self.agent.error_evaluator.last_error.total)

        gaps = self.agent.drive_engine.last_gaps
        self.drive_gap.labels('safe').set(gaps.safe)
        self.drive_gap.labels('gain').set(gaps.gain)
        self.drive_gap.labels('eff').set(gaps.eff)

        self.memory_usage.set(self.agent.get_memory_usage())

    def record_task(self, task, duration):
        self.task_count.labels(task.type).inc()
        self.task_duration.observe(duration)

    def start(self, port=9090):
        start_http_server(port)
```

**日志聚合（ELK）**：

```python
import logging
from logstash_async.handler import AsynchronousLogstashHandler

class ELKIntegration:
    def __init__(self, host, port):
        self.host = host
        self.port = port

    def setup_logging(self):
        handler = AsynchronousLogstashHandler(
            self.host,
            self.port,
            database_path='logstash.db'
        )

        formatter = logging.Formatter('%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s')
        handler.setFormatter(formatter)

        logger = logging.getLogger('SelfEvolvingAgent')
        logger.addHandler(handler)

        return logger
```

## 第五十一章：高级预测模型架构

### 51.1 世界模型（World Models）

世界模型是智能体对环境的内部模拟，能够预测未来状态并支持规划。

**核心组件**：

```python
class WorldModel:
    def __init__(self, state_dim, action_dim, hidden_dim=256):
        self.state_dim = state_dim
        self.action_dim = action_dim

        # 视觉编码器（将高维观测压缩为低维状态）
        self.vision = VisionEncoder(state_dim, hidden_dim)

        # 记忆模型（RNN维护隐藏状态）
        self.memory = MemoryRNN(hidden_dim, hidden_dim)

        # 动态模型（预测下一状态）
        self.dynamics = DynamicsModel(hidden_dim, action_dim, hidden_dim)

        # 奖励模型（预测即时奖励）
        self.reward = RewardModel(hidden_dim, hidden_dim)

    def encode(self, observation):
        """将观测编码为状态表示"""
        return self.vision(observation)

    def step(self, state, action, hidden):
        """预测下一状态和奖励"""
        next_hidden = self.memory(state, action, hidden)
        next_state = self.dynamics(next_hidden)
        reward = self.reward(next_hidden)
        return next_state, reward, next_hidden

    def imagine(self, initial_state, initial_hidden, policy, horizon=50):
        """在想象中执行策略，生成轨迹"""
        states = [initial_state]
        rewards = []
        hidden = initial_hidden

        for _ in range(horizon):
            action = policy(states[-1])
            next_state, reward, hidden = self.step(states[-1], action, hidden)
            states.append(next_state)
            rewards.append(reward)

        return states, rewards
```

**训练策略**：

世界模型的训练分为两个阶段：

1. **监督学习阶段**：使用真实经验训练视觉编码器、记忆模型和动态模型
2. **强化学习阶段**：在想象的环境中训练策略

```python
def train_world_model(agent, env, epochs=1000):
    # 阶段1：收集经验并训练世界模型
    for epoch in range(epochs // 2):
        experience = collect_experience(agent, env)
        agent.world_model.train_on_experience(experience)

    # 阶段2：在想象中训练策略
    for epoch in range(epochs // 2):
        # 从真实经验中采样初始状态
        initial_state, initial_hidden = sample_initial_state(agent.memory)

        # 在想象中生成轨迹
        imagined_trajectory = agent.world_model.imagine(
            initial_state, initial_hidden, agent.policy
        )

        # 使用想象的轨迹训练策略
        agent.policy.train_on_imagined(imagined_trajectory)
```

### 51.2 因果推理模型

因果推理模型能够识别变量之间的因果关系，而不仅仅是相关性。

**因果图学习**：

```python
class CausalDiscovery:
    def __init__(self, variables):
        self.variables = variables
        self.causal_graph = nx.DiGraph()
        self.causal_graph.add_nodes_from(variables)

    def discover(self, data, method='pc'):
        if method == 'pc':
            return self.pc_algorithm(data)
        elif method == 'ges':
            return self.ges_algorithm(data)
        elif method == 'lingam':
            return self.lingam_algorithm(data)

    def pc_algorithm(self, data, alpha=0.05):
        """PC算法：基于条件独立性测试"""
        n = len(self.variables)

        # 步骤1：完全连接图
        graph = nx.complete_graph(self.variables)

        # 步骤2：基于条件独立性测试删除边
        for i in range(n):
            for j in range(i+1, n):
                var_i = self.variables[i]
                var_j = self.variables[j]

                # 测试无条件独立性
                if self.independence_test(data, var_i, var_j, [], alpha):
                    graph.remove_edge(var_i, var_j)
                    continue

                # 测试条件独立性
                for k in range(n):
                    if k != i and k != j:
                        cond = [self.variables[k]]
                        if self.independence_test(data, var_i, var_j, cond, alpha):
                            graph.remove_edge(var_i, var_j)
                            break

        # 步骤3：定向边
        self.orient_edges(graph, data)

        return graph

    def independence_test(self, data, x, y, cond, alpha):
        """条件独立性测试（使用偏相关）"""
        if not cond:
            corr, p_value = pearsonr(data[x], data[y])
        else:
            # 计算偏相关
            residuals_x = self.residuals(data[x], [data[c] for c in cond])
            residuals_y = self.residuals(data[y], [data[c] for c in cond])
            corr, p_value = pearsonr(residuals_x, residuals_y)

        return p_value > alpha

    def orient_edges(self, graph, data):
        """基于v-结构定向边"""
        for node in graph.nodes():
            neighbors = list(graph.neighbors(node))
            for i in range(len(neighbors)):
                for j in range(i+1, len(neighbors)):
                    # 检查v-结构
                    if not graph.has_edge(neighbors[i], neighbors[j]):
                        # 定向为 neighbors[i] -> node <- neighbors[j]
                        self.causal_graph.add_edge(neighbors[i], node)
                        self.causal_graph.add_edge(neighbors[j], node)
```

**因果效应估计**：

```python
class CausalEffectEstimator:
    def __init__(self, causal_graph):
        self.graph = causal_graph

    def estimate_ate(self, treatment, outcome, data, method='backdoor'):
        """估计平均处理效应（ATE）"""
        if method == 'backdoor':
            return self.backdoor_adjustment(treatment, outcome, data)
        elif method == 'frontdoor':
            return self.frontdoor_adjustment(treatment, outcome, data)
        elif method == 'iv':
            return self.instrumental_variable(treatment, outcome, data)

    def backdoor_adjustment(self, treatment, outcome, data):
        """后门调整"""
        # 找到满足后门准则的变量集
        adjustment_set = self.find_backdoor_set(treatment, outcome)

        # 计算调整后的因果效应
        if not adjustment_set:
            # 无混杂变量，直接比较
            treated = data[data[treatment] == 1][outcome].mean()
            control = data[data[treatment] == 0][outcome].mean()
            return treated - control

        # 分层计算
        effect = 0
        for stratum in self.get_strata(data, adjustment_set):
            treated = stratum[stratum[treatment] == 1][outcome].mean()
            control = stratum[stratum[treatment] == 0][outcome].mean()
            weight = len(stratum) / len(data)
            effect += weight * (treated - control)

        return effect

    def find_backdoor_set(self, treatment, outcome):
        """找到满足后门准则的调整变量集"""
        # 找到所有从treatment到outcome的后门路径
        backdoor_paths = self.find_backdoor_paths(treatment, outcome)

        # 找到能够阻断所有后门路径的变量集
        adjustment_set = set()
        for path in backdoor_paths:
            # 路径上的变量（不包括treatment和outcome）
            path_vars = path[1:-1]
            adjustment_set.update(path_vars)

        return adjustment_set
```

### 51.3 元学习模型

元学习（学习如何学习）使Agent能够快速适应新任务。

**模型无关元学习（MAML）**：

```python
class MAML:
    def __init__(self, model, meta_lr=0.001, inner_lr=0.01, inner_steps=5):
        self.model = model
        self.meta_lr = meta_lr
        self.inner_lr = inner_lr
        self.inner_steps = inner_steps
        self.meta_optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=meta_lr)

    def inner_loop(self, task_data):
        """在单个任务上进行内循环优化"""
        # 克隆模型参数
        fast_weights = [p.clone() for p in self.model.parameters()]

        # 内循环梯度下降
        for step in range(self.inner_steps):
            loss = self.compute_loss(self.model, task_data['train'], fast_weights)
            grads = torch.autograd.grad(loss, fast_weights, create_graph=True)
            fast_weights = [w - self.inner_lr * g for w, g in zip(fast_weights, grads)]

        return fast_weights

    def meta_step(self, batch_tasks):
        """元优化步骤"""
        meta_loss = 0

        for task in batch_tasks:
            # 内循环
            fast_weights = self.inner_loop(task)

            # 在测试集上评估
            test_loss = self.compute_loss(self.model, task['test'], fast_weights)
            meta_loss += test_loss

        # 元梯度下降
        self.meta_optimizer.zero_grad()
        meta_loss.backward()
        self.meta_optimizer.step()

        return meta_loss.item()

    def adapt(self, new_task_data):
        """快速适应新任务"""
        # 使用内循环进行快速适应
        adapted_weights = self.inner_loop(new_task_data)

        # 更新模型参数
        for p, w in zip(self.model.parameters(), adapted_weights):
            p.data = w.data
```

**基于记忆的元学习**：

```python
class MemoryAugmentedMetaLearning:
    def __init__(self, model, memory_size=128, memory_dim=64):
        self.model = model
        self.memory = Neural TuringMachine(memory_size, memory_dim)

    def forward(self, x, context):
        # 从记忆中读取相关信息
        memory_output = self.memory.read(context)

        # 结合输入和记忆信息
        combined = torch.cat([x, memory_output], dim=-1)

        # 生成预测
        prediction = self.model(combined)

        return prediction

    def update_memory(self, context, target):
        # 将新信息写入记忆
        self.memory.write(context, target)
```

## 第五十二章：高级驱力与动机系统

### 52.1 好奇心驱动的探索

**基于预测误差的好奇心**：

```python
class CuriosityDrive:
    def __init__(self, forward_model, inverse_model):
        self.forward_model = forward_model  # 预测下一状态
        self.inverse_model = inverse_model  # 预测动作

    def compute_intrinsic_reward(self, state, action, next_state):
        # 前向模型预测下一状态
        predicted_next = self.forward_model(state, action)

        # 预测误差作为内在奖励
        prediction_error = F.mse_loss(predicted_next, next_state)

        return prediction_error.item()
```

**基于信息增益的好奇心**：

```python
class InformationGainCuriosity:
    def __init__(self, ensemble_model):
        self.ensemble = ensemble_model  # 集成模型

    def compute_intrinsic_reward(self, state, action):
        # 获取集成模型的多个预测
        predictions = self.ensemble.predict_all(state, action)

        # 预测方差作为不确定性度量
        uncertainty = torch.var(predictions, dim=0).mean()

        return uncertainty.item()
```

**基于状态新颖性的好奇心**：

```python
class NoveltySeeking:
    def __init__(self, state_encoder, density_model):
        self.encoder = state_encoder
        self.density = density_model  # 状态密度模型

    def compute_intrinsic_reward(self, state):
        # 编码状态
        encoded = self.encoder(state)

        # 计算状态密度
        density = self.density.probability(encoded)

        # 密度越低（越新颖），奖励越高
        novelty = -torch.log(density + 1e-8)

        # 更新密度模型
        self.density.update(encoded)

        return novelty.item()
```

### 52.2 社会驱力

**模仿学习**：

```python
class ImitationDrive:
    def __init__(self, expert_policy):
        self.expert = expert_policy
        self.imitation_model = None

    def compute_reward(self, state, action):
        # 专家动作
        expert_action = self.expert(state)

        # 模仿误差
        imitation_error = F.mse_loss(action, expert_action)

        return -imitation_error.item()

    def learn_from_expert(self, expert_trajectories):
        # 训练模仿模型
        self.imitation_model = BehavioralCloning()
        self.imitation_model.train(expert_trajectories)
```

**竞争驱力**：

```python
class CompetitiveDrive:
    def __init__(self, opponent):
        self.opponent = opponent
        self.win_count = 0
        self.total_games = 0

    def compute_reward(self, outcome):
        self.total_games += 1

        if outcome == 'win':
            self.win_count += 1
            return 1.0
        elif outcome == 'draw':
            return 0.0
        else:
            return -1.0

    def get_win_rate(self):
        return self.win_count / max(self.total_games, 1)
```

class CombinatorialCreativity:
    def __init__(self, concept_space):
        self.concepts = concept_space
        self.combinations = []

    def generate_novel_combination(self):
        # 随机选择两个概念
        concept_a = random.choice(self.concepts)
        concept_b = random.choice(self.concepts)

        # 组合概念
        combination = self.combine(concept_a, concept_b)

        # 检查新颖性
        if self.is_novel(combination):
            self.combinations.append(combination)
            return combination

        return None

    def combine(self, a, b):
        # 概念组合操作
        return {
            'components': [a, b],
            'features': a['features'] + b['features'],
            'relations': self.infer_relations(a, b)
        }

## 第五十三章：高级学习算法

### 53.1 持续学习

**弹性权重巩固（EWC）**：

```python
class EWC:
    def __init__(self, model, lambda_ewc=1000):
        self.model = model
        self.lambda_ewc = lambda_ewc
        self.fisher_dict = {}  # Fisher信息矩阵
        self.optimal_params = {}  # 最优参数

    def compute_fisher(self, dataloader):
        """计算Fisher信息矩阵"""
        fisher = {n: torch.zeros_like(p) for n, p in self.model.named_parameters()}

        for batch in dataloader:
            self.model.zero_grad()
            output = self.model(batch)
            loss = F.nll_loss(output, batch.target)
            loss.backward()

            for n, p in self.model.named_parameters():
                if p.grad is not None:
                    fisher[n] += p.grad.pow(2) / len(dataloader)

        return fisher

    def update_ewc_params(self, task_id, dataloader):
        """更新EWC参数"""
        self.fisher_dict[task_id] = self.compute_fisher(dataloader)
        self.optimal_params[task_id] = {
            n: p.clone().detach() for n, p in self.model.named_parameters()
        }

    def penalty(self):
        """计算EWC惩罚项"""
        loss = 0
        for task_id in self.fisher_dict:
            for n, p in self.model.named_parameters():
                if n in self.fisher_dict[task_id]:
                    fisher = self.fisher_dict[task_id][n]
                    optimal = self.optimal_params[task_id][n]
                    loss += (fisher * (p - optimal).pow(2)).sum()

        return self.lambda_ewc * loss

    def train(self, dataloader, task_id):
        for batch in dataloader:
            self.model.zero_grad()
            output = self.model(batch)
            loss = F.nll_loss(output, batch.target) + self.penalty()
            loss.backward()
            self.optimizer.step()
```

**渐进神经网络**：

```python
class ProgressiveNeuralNetwork:
    def __init__(self, input_size, output_size, hidden_size=256):
        self.columns = []
        self.input_size = input_size
        self.output_size = output_size
        self.hidden_size = hidden_size

    def add_column(self, task_id):
        """为新任务添加新列"""
        column = nn.ModuleDict({
            'h': nn.Linear(self.input_size, self.hidden_size),
            'out': nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.output_size)
        })

        # 添加横向连接
        for prev_column in self.columns:
            lateral = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.hidden_size)
            column[f'lateral_{len(self.columns)}'] = lateral

        self.columns.append(column)

    def forward(self, x, task_id):
        """前向传播，使用所有列"""
        column = self.columns[task_id]

        # 基础隐藏状态
        h = F.relu(column['h'](x))

        # 添加横向连接
        for i, prev_column in enumerate(self.columns[:task_id]):
            prev_h = F.relu(prev_column['h'](x))
            lateral = column[f'lateral_{i}'](prev_h)
            h = h + lateral

        # 输出
        output = column['out'](h)
        return output
```

### 53.2 分层强化学习

**选项框架（Options Framework）**：

```python
class Option:
    def __init__(self, initiation_set, policy, termination_condition):
        self.initiation_set = initiation_set  # 可启动该选项的状态集
        self.policy = policy  # 选项内部策略
        self.termination = termination_condition  # 终止条件

    def can_initiate(self, state):
        return state in self.initiation_set

    def execute(self, env, state):
        """执行选项直到终止"""
        trajectory = []
        current_state = state

        while not self.termination(current_state):
            action = self.policy(current_state)
            next_state, reward, done, _ = env.step(action)
            trajectory.append((current_state, action, reward))
            current_state = next_state

            if done:
                break

        return trajectory, current_state

class HierarchicalRL:
    def __init__(self, primitive_actions, options):
        self.primitive_actions = primitive_actions
        self.options = options
        self.meta_policy = None  # 高层策略（选择选项或原始动作）

    def select_action(self, state):
        # 高层决策：选择选项或原始动作
        available_options = [o for o in self.options if o.can_initiate(state)]
        all_choices = self.primitive_actions + available_options

        return self.meta_policy(state, all_choices)

    def train(self, env, episodes):
        for episode in episodes:
            state = env.reset()
            done = False

            while not done:
                choice = self.select_action(state)

                if isinstance(choice, Option):
                    # 执行选项
                    trajectory, state = choice.execute(env, state)
                    # 使用选项内部的经验训练选项策略
                    choice.policy.train(trajectory)
                else:
                    # 执行原始动作
                    next_state, reward, done, _ = env.step(choice)
                    state = next_state
```

**封建制强化学习**：

```python
class FeudalRL:
    def __init__(self, state_dim, n_managers, n_workers):
        self.managers = [Manager(state_dim) for _ in range(n_managers)]
        self.workers = [Worker(state_dim) for _ in range(n_workers)]

    def act(self, state):
        # 管理者设定目标
        goals = [manager.set_goal(state) for manager in self.managers]

        # 工作者执行动作以实现目标
        actions = []
        for worker, goal in zip(self.workers, goals):
            action = worker.act(state, goal)
            actions.append(action)

        return actions

class Manager:
    def __init__(self, state_dim, goal_dim=16):
        self.goal_network = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(state_dim, 128),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Linear(128, goal_dim)
        )
        self.goal_period = 10  # 目标持续时间

    def set_goal(self, state):
        goal = self.goal_network(state)
        return goal

class Worker:
    def __init__(self, state_dim, action_dim, goal_dim=16):
        self.policy = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(state_dim + goal_dim, 128),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Linear(128, action_dim)
        )

    def act(self, state, goal):
        x = torch.cat([state, goal], dim=-1)
        action_probs = F.softmax(self.policy(x), dim=-1)
        action = torch.multinomial(action_probs, 1)
        return action
```

### 53.3 离线强化学习

**保守Q学习（CQL）**：

```python
class CQL:
    def __init__(self, q_network, policy, alpha=1.0):
        self.q_network = q_network
        self.policy = policy
        self.alpha = alpha

    def cql_loss(self, q_values, dataset_q_values):
        """CQL正则化项"""
        # 数据集Q值
        dataset_q = dataset_q_values.mean()

        # 所有动作的Q值（logsumexp）
        all_q = torch.logsumexp(q_values, dim=-1).mean()

        # CQL损失：鼓励数据集动作的Q值高，其他动作的Q值低
        cql_loss = all_q - dataset_q

        return cql_loss

    def train(self, dataset, batch_size=256):
        for batch in DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=batch_size):
            state, action, reward, next_state, done = batch

            # 标准贝尔曼误差
            q_values = self.q_network(state)
            q_value = q_values.gather(1, action.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)

            with torch.no_grad():
                next_q = self.q_network(next_state).max(1)[0]
                target_q = reward + 0.99 * next_q * (1 - done)

            bellman_loss = F.mse_loss(q_value, target_q)

            # CQL正则化
            cql_loss = self.cql_loss(q_values, q_value)

            # 总损失
            total_loss = bellman_loss + self.alpha * cql_loss

            # 优化
            self.q_optimizer.zero_grad()
            total_loss.backward()
            self.q_optimizer.step()
```

## 第五十四章：神经符号集成

### 54.1 可微分逻辑

**逻辑神经网络**：

```python
class LogicNN:
    def __init__(self, predicates, rules):
        self.predicates = predicates
        self.rules = rules

        # 为每个规则学习权重
        self.rule_weights = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(len(rules)))

    def forward(self, facts):
        """前向推理"""
        # 初始化已知事实
        beliefs = facts.clone()

        # 迭代应用规则
        for iteration in range(10):
            new_beliefs = beliefs.clone()

            for i, rule in enumerate(self.rules):
                # 计算规则触发度
                trigger = self.evaluate_rule(rule, beliefs)

                # 加权更新
                weight = torch.sigmoid(self.rule_weights[i])
                conclusion = rule.conclusion
                new_beliefs[conclusion] += weight * trigger

            # 收敛检查
            if torch.allclose(beliefs, new_beliefs, atol=1e-6):
                break

            beliefs = new_beliefs

        return torch.sigmoid(beliefs)

    def evaluate_rule(self, rule, beliefs):
        """评估规则的触发程度"""
        if rule.type == 'AND':
            return torch.min(beliefs[rule.premises])
        elif rule.type == 'OR':
            return torch.max(beliefs[rule.premises])
        elif rule.type == 'NOT':
            return 1 - beliefs[rule.premises[0]]
```

**神经定理证明**：

```python
class NeuralTheoremProver:
    def __init__(self, knowledge_base):
        self.kb = knowledge_base
        self.unification_net = UnificationNetwork()
        self.proof_policy = ProofPolicyNetwork()

    def prove(self, goal, max_depth=10):
        """尝试证明目标"""
        proof_state = ProofState(goal)
        proof_trace = []

        for step in range(max_depth):
            # 选择下一个证明步骤
            action = self.proof_policy.select_action(proof_state)

            if action.type == 'unify':
                # 尝试与知识库中的事实统一
                success, substitution = self.unification_net.unify(
                    proof_state.current_goal,
                    action.target
                )

                if success:
                    proof_state.apply_substitution(substitution)
                    proof_trace.append(('unify', action.target, substitution))
                else:
                    proof_trace.append(('fail', action.target))

            elif action.type == 'resolve':
                # 使用规则进行归结
                new_goals = self.resolve(proof_state.current_goal, action.rule)
                proof_state.push_goals(new_goals)
                proof_trace.append(('resolve', action.rule, new_goals))

            elif action.type == 'success':
                return True, proof_trace

            if proof_state.is_empty():
                return True, proof_trace

        return False, proof_trace
```

### 54.2 神经程序合成

**可微分编程**：

```python
class DifferentiableProgram:
    def __init__(self, operations, controller):
        self.operations = operations  # 可微分操作库
        self.controller = controller  # 控制器（选择操作和参数）

    def execute(self, input_data, program_length=10):
        """执行程序"""
        state = input_data
        trace = []

        for step in range(program_length):
            # 控制器选择操作
            op_logits, arg_logits = self.controller(state)
            op_probs = F.softmax(op_logits, dim=-1)
            arg_probs = F.softmax(arg_logits, dim=-1)

            # 软选择操作（可微分）
            op_embedding = torch.matmul(op_probs, self.operation_embeddings)
            arg_embedding = torch.matmul(arg_probs, self.argument_embeddings)

            # 执行操作（可微分近似）
            next_state = self.soft_execute(state, op_embedding, arg_embedding)

            trace.append({
                'op_probs': op_probs,
                'arg_probs': arg_probs,
                'state': next_state
            })

            state = next_state

        return state, trace

    def soft_execute(self, state, op_embedding, arg_embedding):
        """软执行操作（可微分）"""
        # 对所有操作进行加权求和
        result = torch.zeros_like(state)
        for i, op in enumerate(self.operations):
            weight = op_embedding[i]
            op_result = op(state, arg_embedding)
            result += weight * op_result

        return result
```

## 第五十五章：量子计算与AI

### 55.1 量子机器学习

**量子神经网络**：

```python
class QuantumNeuralNetwork:
    def __init__(self, n_qubits, n_layers):
        self.n_qubits = n_qubits
        self.n_layers = n_layers
        self.dev = qml.device('default.qubit', wires=n_qubits)

    def build_circuit(self):
        @qml.qnode(self.dev)
        def circuit(inputs, weights):
            # 编码输入
            for i in range(self.n_qubits):
                qml.RY(inputs[i], wires=i)

            # 变分层
            for layer in range(self.n_layers):
                for i in range(self.n_qubits):
                    qml.RX(weights[layer, i, 0], wires=i)
                    qml.RZ(weights[layer, i, 1], wires=i)

                # 纠缠层
                for i in range(self.n_qubits - 1):
                    qml.CNOT(wires=[i, i+1])

            # 测量
            return [qml.expval(qml.PauliZ(i)) for i in range(self.n_qubits)]

        return circuit

    def train(self, X, y, epochs=100):
        circuit = self.build_circuit()
        weights = np.random.randn(self.n_layers, self.n_qubits, 2)
        opt = qml.GradientDescentOptimizer(stepsize=0.01)

        for epoch in range(epochs):
            def cost(w):
                predictions = np.array([circuit(x, w) for x in X])
                return np.mean((predictions - y) ** 2)

            weights = opt.step(cost, weights)

        return weights
```

**量子强化学习**：

```python
class QuantumRL:
    def __init__(self, n_states, n_actions, n_qubits):
        self.n_states = n_states
        self.n_actions = n_actions
        self.n_qubits = n_qubits
        self.dev = qml.device('default.qubit', wires=n_qubits)

    def build_q_value_circuit(self):
        @qml.qnode(self.dev)
        def q_value_circuit(state, weights):
            # 状态编码
            for i in range(min(len(state), self.n_qubits)):
                qml.RY(state[i] * np.pi, wires=i)

            # 变分电路
            for layer in range(len(weights)):
                for i in range(self.n_qubits):
                    qml.RX(weights[layer][i][0], wires=i)
                    qml.RZ(weights[layer][i][1], wires=i)
                for i in range(self.n_qubits - 1):
                    qml.CNOT(wires=[i, i+1])

            # 测量动作价值
            return [qml.expval(qml.PauliZ(i)) for i in range(self.n_actions)]

        return q_value_circuit
```

## 第五十六章：生物启发计算

### 56.1 神经形态计算

**脉冲神经网络（SNN）**：

```python
class SpikingNeuron:
    def __init__(self, tau_mem=20e-3, tau_syn=5e-3, v_thresh=1.0, v_reset=0.0):
        self.tau_mem = tau_mem  # 膜时间常数
        self.tau_syn = tau_syn  # 突触时间常数
        self.v_thresh = v_thresh  # 阈值电压
        self.v_reset = v_reset  # 重置电压

        self.v = v_reset  # 膜电位
        self.i = 0.0  # 突触电流

    def update(self, dt, input_current):
        # 更新突触电流
        di = -self.i / self.tau_syn + input_current
        self.i += di * dt

        # 更新膜电位
        dv = (-self.v + self.i) / self.tau_mem
        self.v += dv * dt

        # 检查是否发放脉冲
        spike = self.v >= self.v_thresh
        if spike:
            self.v = self.v_reset

        return spike

class SpikingNeuralNetwork:
    def __init__(self, layers):
        self.layers = []
        for i in range(len(layers) - 1):
            layer = [[SpikingNeuron() for _ in range(layers[i+1])]
                     for _ in range(layers[i])]
            self.layers.append(layer)

    def forward(self, spike_train, T=100):
        """前向传播"""
        dt = 1e-3  # 1ms时间步
        spikes = spike_train

        for layer in self.layers:
            output_spikes = []
            for t in range(T):
                # 计算突触输入
                synaptic_input = self.compute_synaptic_input(spikes, layer)

                # 更新神经元
                layer_spikes = []
                for neuron in layer:
                    spike = neuron.update(dt, synaptic_input)
                    layer_spikes.append(spike)

                output_spikes.append(layer_spikes)

            spikes = output_spikes

        return spikes
```

### 56.2 群体智能

**粒子群优化（PSO）**：

```python
class ParticleSwarmOptimization:
    def __init__(self, n_particles, dim, w=0.7, c1=1.5, c2=1.5):
        self.n_particles = n_particles
        self.dim = dim
        self.w = w  # 惯性权重
        self.c1 = c1  # 认知系数
        self.c2 = c2  # 社会系数

        # 初始化粒子
        self.positions = np.random.randn(n_particles, dim)
        self.velocities = np.random.randn(n_particles, dim) * 0.1

        # 个体最优
        self.pbest = self.positions.copy()
        self.pbest_fitness = np.full(n_particles, float('inf'))

        # 全局最优
        self.gbest = None
        self.gbest_fitness = float('inf')

    def optimize(self, fitness_func, max_iter=100):
        for iteration in range(max_iter):
            # 评估适应度
            fitness = np.array([fitness_func(p) for p in self.positions])

            # 更新个体最优
            improved = fitness < self.pbest_fitness
            self.pbest[improved] = self.positions[improved]
            self.pbest_fitness[improved] = fitness[improved]

            # 更新全局最优
            best_idx = np.argmin(fitness)
            if fitness[best_idx] < self.gbest_fitness:
                self.gbest = self.positions[best_idx].copy()
                self.gbest_fitness = fitness[best_idx]

            # 更新速度和位置
            r1, r2 = np.random.rand(2)
            self.velocities = (
                self.w * self.velocities
                + self.c1 * r1 * (self.pbest - self.positions)
                + self.c2 * r2 * (self.gbest - self.positions)
            )
            self.positions += self.velocities

            print(f"Iteration {iteration}: Best fitness = {self.gbest_fitness}")

        return self.gbest, self.gbest_fitness
```

**蚁群优化（ACO）**：

```python
class AntColonyOptimization:
    def __init__(self, n_ants, n_nodes, alpha=1.0, beta=2.0, rho=0.5):
        self.n_ants = n_ants
        self.n_nodes = n_nodes
        self.alpha = alpha  # 信息素重要性
        self.beta = beta  # 启发式信息重要性
        self.rho = rho  # 信息素蒸发率

        # 初始化信息素
        self.pheromone = np.ones((n_nodes, n_nodes))

    def solve_tsp(self, distance_matrix, max_iter=100):
        best_tour = None
        best_length = float('inf')

        for iteration in range(max_iter):
            all_tours = []
            all_lengths = []

            for ant in range(self.n_ants):
                tour = self.construct_tour(distance_matrix)
                length = self.tour_length(tour, distance_matrix)
                all_tours.append(tour)
                all_lengths.append(length)

                if length < best_length:
                    best_length = length
                    best_tour = tour

            # 更新信息素
            self.update_pheromone(all_tours, all_lengths)

            print(f"Iteration {iteration}: Best length = {best_length}")

        return best_tour, best_length

    def construct_tour(self, distance_matrix):
        tour = [np.random.randint(self.n_nodes)]
        unvisited = set(range(self.n_nodes)) - {tour[0]}

        while unvisited:
            current = tour[-1]
            probabilities = []

            for node in unvisited:
                tau = self.pheromone[current][node] ** self.alpha
                eta = (1.0 / distance_matrix[current][node]) ** self.beta
                probabilities.append(tau * eta)

            probabilities = np.array(probabilities)
            probabilities /= probabilities.sum()

            next_node = np.random.choice(list(unvisited), p=probabilities)
            tour.append(next_node)
            unvisited.remove(next_node)

        return tour

    def update_pheromone(self, tours, lengths):
        # 信息素蒸发
        self.pheromone *= (1 - self.rho)

        # 信息素沉积
        for tour, length in zip(tours, lengths):
            deposit = 1.0 / length
            for i in range(len(tour)):
                j = (i + 1) % len(tour)
                self.pheromone[tour[i]][tour[j]] += deposit
```

## 第五十七章：伦理与治理

### 57.1 AI伦理框架

**公平性评估**：

```python
class FairnessEvaluator:
    def __init__(self, predictions, labels, protected_attributes):
        self.predictions = predictions
        self.labels = labels
        self.protected = protected_attributes

    def demographic_parity(self):
        """人口统计均等"""
        groups = np.unique(self.protected)
        rates = []

        for group in groups:
            mask = self.protected == group
            rate = self.predictions[mask].mean()
            rates.append(rate)

        return max(rates) - min(rates)

    def equal_opportunity(self):
        """机会均等"""
        groups = np.unique(self.protected)
        tprs = []

        for group in groups:
            mask = (self.protected == group) & (self.labels == 1)
            if mask.sum() > 0:
                tpr = self.predictions[mask].mean()
                tprs.append(tpr)

        return max(tprs) - min(tprs)

    def calibration(self):
        """校准性"""
        groups = np.unique(self.protected)
        calibration_scores = []

        for group in groups:
            mask = self.protected == group
            pred_probs = self.predictions[mask]
            true_labels = self.labels[mask]

            # 按预测概率分桶
            bins = np.linspace(0, 1, 11)
            for i in range(len(bins) - 1):
                bin_mask = (pred_probs >= bins[i]) & (pred_probs < bins[i+1])
                if bin_mask.sum() > 0:
                    avg_pred = pred_probs[bin_mask].mean()
                    avg_true = true_labels[bin_mask].mean()
                    calibration_scores.append(abs(avg_pred - avg_true))

        return np.mean(calibration_scores)
```

**可解释性方法**：

```python
class Explainability:
    def __init__(self, model):
        self.model = model

    def lime_explanation(self, instance, num_features=10):
        """LIME局部解释"""
        # 在实例附近采样
        samples = self.perturb_instance(instance)

        # 获取预测
        predictions = self.model.predict(samples)

        # 训练局部线性模型
        weights = self.compute_weights(instance, samples)
        local_model = Ridge(alpha=1.0)
        local_model.fit(samples, predictions, sample_weight=weights)

        # 获取最重要的特征
        feature_importance = np.argsort(np.abs(local_model.coef_))[-num_features:]

        return feature_importance, local_model.coef_[feature_importance]

    def shap_explanation(self, instance, background_data):
        """SHAP值解释"""
        explainer = shap.KernelExplainer(self.model.predict, background_data)
        shap_values = explainer.shap_values(instance)

        return shap_values

    def attention_visualization(self, input_data):
        """注意力权重可视化"""
        attention_weights = self.model.get_attention_weights(input_data)

        # 可视化注意力热力图
        plt.figure(figsize=(10, 8))
        sns.heatmap(attention_weights, cmap='viridis')
        plt.title('Attention Weights')
        plt.show()

        return attention_weights
```

### 57.2 治理机制

**价值对齐**：

```python
class ValueAlignment:
    def __init__(self, human_values):
        self.human_values = human_values
        self.value_model = self.build_value_model()

    def build_value_model(self):
        """构建人类价值模型"""
        # 从人类反馈中学习价值函数
        model = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(self.state_dim, 256),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Linear(256, 128),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Linear(128, len(self.human_values))
        )
        return model

    def human_preference_learning(self, comparisons):
        """从人类比较中学习"""
        optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.value_model.parameters())

        for comparison in comparisons:
            trajectory_a = comparison['trajectory_a']
            trajectory_b = comparison['trajectory_b']
            preference = comparison['preference']  # 0: a preferred, 1: b preferred, 0.5: tie

            # 计算价值
            value_a = self.value_model(trajectory_a).sum()
            value_b = self.value_model(trajectory_b).sum()

            # Bradley-Terry模型
            prob_b_preferred = torch.sigmoid(value_b - value_a)

            # 损失
            if preference == 0.5:
                loss = -torch.log(1 - prob_b_preferred + 1e-8) * 0.5 - torch.log(prob_b_preferred + 1e-8) * 0.5
            else:
                loss = -torch.log(prob_b_preferred + 1e-8) if preference == 1 else -torch.log(1 - prob_b_preferred + 1e-8)

            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()

    def evaluate_alignment(self, policy):
        """评估策略与人类价值的一致性"""
        trajectories = policy.generate_trajectories(100)
        values = [self.value_model(t).mean().item() for t in trajectories]
        return np.mean(values)
```

## 第五十九章：分布式Agent系统

### 59.1 多Agent架构

**联邦式多Agent系统**：

```python
class FederatedAgentSystem:
    def __init__(self, coordinator, agents):
        self.coordinator = coordinator
        self.agents = agents
        self.global_model = None

    def federated_learning_round(self):
        """执行一轮联邦学习"""
        # 1. 分发全局模型
        for agent in self.agents:
            agent.receive_global_model(self.global_model)

        # 2. 本地训练
        local_updates = []
        for agent in self.agents:
            update = agent.local_train()
            local_updates.append(update)

        # 3. 聚合更新
        self.global_model = self.aggregate_updates(local_updates)

        return self.global_model

    def aggregate_updates(self, updates):
        """聚合本地更新（FedAvg）"""
        total_samples = sum(u['n_samples'] for u in updates)
        aggregated = {}

        for key in updates[0]['weights']:
            aggregated[key] = sum(
                u['weights'][key] * u['n_samples'] / total_samples
                for u in updates
            )

        return aggregated

class FederatedAgent:
    def __init__(self, agent_id, local_data):
        self.agent_id = agent_id
        self.local_data = local_data
        self.local_model = None

    def receive_global_model(self, global_model):
        self.local_model = copy.deepcopy(global_model)

    def local_train(self, epochs=5):
        """本地训练"""
        optimizer = torch.optim.SGD(self.local_model.parameters(), lr=0.01)

        for epoch in range(epochs):
            for batch in self.local_data:
                optimizer.zero_grad()
                loss = self.compute_loss(batch)
                loss.backward()
                optimizer.step()

        return {
            'weights': self.local_model.state_dict(),
            'n_samples': len(self.local_data)
        }
```

**共识机制**：

```python
class ConsensusProtocol:
    def __init__(self, agents, threshold=0.67):
        self.agents = agents
        self.threshold = threshold

    def reach_consensus(self, proposals):
        """通过投票达成共识"""
        votes = defaultdict(int)

        for agent in self.agents:
            vote = agent.vote(proposals)
            votes[vote] += 1

        total = len(self.agents)
        for proposal, count in votes.items():
            if count / total >= self.threshold:
                return proposal

        return None  # 未达成共识

    def byzantine_fault_tolerance(self, proposals, max_faulty):
        """拜占庭容错共识"""
        # PBFT算法简化版
        n = len(self.agents)
        f = max_faulty

        if n < 3 * f + 1:
            raise ValueError("Insufficient agents for BFT")

        # 预准备阶段
        pre_prepare = self.select_primary().propose(proposals)

        # 准备阶段
        prepare_votes = []
        for agent in self.agents:
            if agent.verify(pre_prepare):
                prepare_votes.append(agent.sign(pre_prepare))

        if len(prepare_votes) < 2 * f:
            return None

        # 提交阶段
        commit_votes = []
        for agent in self.agents:
            if agent.verify_prepare_certificate(prepare_votes):
                commit_votes.append(agent.sign_commit(pre_prepare))

        if len(commit_votes) < 2 * f + 1:
            return None

        return pre_prepare
```

### 59.2 通信协议

**Agent通信语言（ACL）**：

```python
class ACLMessage:
    def __init__(self):
        self.performative = None  # 言语行为类型
        self.sender = None
        self.receiver = None
        self.content = None
        self.language = None
        self.ontology = None
        self.conversation_id = None
        self.reply_with = None
        self.in_reply_to = None

    @classmethod
    def inform(cls, sender, receiver, content):
        msg = cls()
        msg.performative = 'INFORM'
        msg.sender = sender
        msg.receiver = receiver
        msg.content = content
        return msg

    @classmethod
    def request(cls, sender, receiver, action):
        msg = cls()
        msg.performative = 'REQUEST'
        msg.sender = sender
        msg.receiver = receiver
        msg.content = action
        return msg

    @classmethod
    def propose(cls, sender, receiver, proposal):
        msg = cls()
        msg.performative = 'PROPOSE'
        msg.sender = sender
        msg.receiver = receiver
        msg.content = proposal
        return msg

class CommunicationProtocol:
    def __init__(self, agent):
        self.agent = agent
        self.inbox = Queue()
        self.outbox = Queue()
        self.conversations = {}

    def send(self, message):
        """发送消息"""
        self.outbox.put(message)

    def receive(self, timeout=None):
        """接收消息"""
        try:
            return self.inbox.get(timeout=timeout)
        except Empty:
            return None

    def handle_message(self, message):
        """处理收到的消息"""
        handler = self.get_handler(message.performative)
        return handler(message)

    def get_handler(self, performative):
        handlers = {
            'INFORM': self.handle_inform,
            'REQUEST': self.handle_request,
            'PROPOSE': self.handle_propose,
            'ACCEPT': self.handle_accept,
            'REJECT': self.handle_reject,
            'QUERY': self.handle_query
        }
        return handlers.get(performative, self.handle_unknown)

    def handle_request(self, message):
        """处理请求"""
        action = message.content
        if self.agent.can_execute(action):
            result = self.agent.execute(action)
            reply = ACLMessage.inform(
                self.agent.id,
                message.sender,
                result
            )
            self.send(reply)
        else:
            reply = ACLMessage.reject(
                self.agent.id,
                message.sender,
                'Cannot execute action'
            )
            self.send(reply)
```

## 第六十章：对抗鲁棒性

### 60.1 对抗攻击防御

**对抗训练**：

```python
class AdversarialTraining:
    def __init__(self, model, epsilon=0.03):
        self.model = model
        self.epsilon = epsilon

    def generate_adversarial_example(self, x, y, method='pgd'):
        """生成对抗样本"""
        if method == 'fgsm':
            return self.fgsm_attack(x, y)
        elif method == 'pgd':
            return self.pgd_attack(x, y)

    def fgsm_attack(self, x, y):
        """快速梯度符号攻击"""
        x_adv = x.clone().detach().requires_grad_(True)
        output = self.model(x_adv)
        loss = F.cross_entropy(output, y)

        self.model.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()

        # 生成对抗样本
        x_adv = x_adv + self.epsilon * x_adv.grad.sign()
        x_adv = torch.clamp(x_adv, 0, 1)

        return x_adv.detach()

    def pgd_attack(self, x, y, steps=10, step_size=0.01):
        """投影梯度下降攻击"""
        x_adv = x.clone().detach() + torch.empty_like(x).uniform_(-self.epsilon, self.epsilon)
        x_adv = torch.clamp(x_adv, 0, 1)

        for _ in range(steps):
            x_adv.requires_grad_(True)
            output = self.model(x_adv)
            loss = F.cross_entropy(output, y)

            self.model.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()

            # 更新对抗样本
            x_adv = x_adv.detach() + step_size * x_adv.grad.sign()
            # 投影到epsilon球内
            perturbation = torch.clamp(x_adv - x, -self.epsilon, self.epsilon)
            x_adv = torch.clamp(x + perturbation, 0, 1)

        return x_adv

    def train(self, dataloader, epochs=10):
        """对抗训练"""
        optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.model.parameters())

        for epoch in range(epochs):
            for batch in dataloader:
                x, y = batch

                # 生成对抗样本
                x_adv = self.generate_adversarial_example(x, y)

                # 在原始样本和对抗样本上训练
                output_clean = self.model(x)
                output_adv = self.model(x_adv)

                loss = (F.cross_entropy(output_clean, y) +
                        F.cross_entropy(output_adv, y)) / 2

                optimizer.zero_grad()
                loss.backward()
                optimizer.step()
```

**输入验证**：

```python
class InputValidator:
    def __init__(self, bounds, constraints):
        self.bounds = bounds
        self.constraints = constraints

    def validate(self, x):
        """验证输入"""
        violations = []

        # 检查边界
        for i, (low, high) in enumerate(self.bounds):
            if x[i] < low or x[i] > high:
                violations.append(f"Dimension {i} out of bounds: [{low}, {high}]")

        # 检查约束
        for constraint in self.constraints:
            if not constraint(x):
                violations.append(f"Constraint violated: {constraint.__name__}")

        # 检查异常值
        if self.is_anomaly(x):
            violations.append("Input detected as anomaly")

        return len(violations) == 0, violations

    def is_anomaly(self, x):
        """使用统计方法检测异常输入"""
        # 基于历史数据的异常检测
        pass

    def sanitize(self, x):
        """清理输入"""
        # 裁剪到边界
        x_clean = np.clip(x, [b[0] for b in self.bounds], [b[1] for b in self.bounds])

        # 添加噪声（随机化防御）
        x_clean += np.random.normal(0, 0.01, size=x_clean.shape)

        return x_clean
```

### 60.2 模型窃取防御

```python
class ModelProtection:
    def __init__(self, model):
        self.model = model
        self.query_history = []
        self.max_queries_per_client = 1000

    def protected_predict(self, x, client_id):
        """受保护的预测接口"""
        # 检查查询频率
        if not self.check_query_limit(client_id):
            raise PermissionError("Query limit exceeded")

        # 记录查询
        self.query_history.append({
            'client_id': client_id,
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'input_hash': hash(x.tobytes())
        })

        # 添加噪声到输出
        output = self.model(x)
        noisy_output = self.add_output_noise(output)

        return noisy_output

    def check_query_limit(self, client_id):
        """检查查询限制"""
        recent_queries = [
            q for q in self.query_history
            if q['client_id'] == client_id and
            time.time() - q['timestamp'] < 3600
        ]
        return len(recent_queries) < self.max_queries_per_client

    def add_output_noise(self, output):
        """添加输出噪声"""
        noise = torch.randn_like(output) * 0.01
        return output + noise

    def detect_model_stealing(self):
        """检测模型窃取攻击"""
        # 分析查询模式
        query_patterns = self.analyze_query_patterns()

        # 检测异常模式
        suspicious_clients = []
        for client_id, pattern in query_patterns.items():
            if self.is_suspicious_pattern(pattern):
                suspicious_clients.append(client_id)

        return suspicious_clients

    def is_suspicious_pattern(self, pattern):
        """判断查询模式是否可疑"""
        # 检查是否覆盖整个输入空间（系统性探测）
        coverage = pattern['input_coverage']
        if coverage > 0.9:
            return True

        # 检查查询的系统性
        systematic_score = pattern['systematic_score']
        if systematic_score > 0.8:
            return True

        return False
```

## 第六十一章：边缘计算与IoT集成

### 61.1 边缘Agent架构

**轻量级模型设计**：

```python
class LightweightModel:
    def __init__(self, input_dim, output_dim, max_params=100000):
        self.input_dim = input_dim
        self.output_dim = output_dim
        self.max_params = max_params

        # 设计满足参数限制的架构
        self.model = self.design_architecture()

    def design_architecture(self):
        """设计满足约束的架构"""
        # 计算可用的隐藏层大小
        remaining_params = self.max_params
        remaining_params -= (self.input_dim * self.output_dim)  # 输出层

        # 尝试添加隐藏层
        layers = []
        hidden_size = 64

        while remaining_params > 0:
            if len(layers) == 0:
                n_params = self.input_dim * hidden_size + hidden_size
            else:
                n_params = layers[-1]['size'] * hidden_size + hidden_size

            if n_params > remaining_params:
                break

            layers.append({
                'size': hidden_size,
                'activation': 'relu'
            })
            remaining_params -= n_params

        # 构建模型
        model = nn.Sequential()
        prev_size = self.input_dim
        for i, layer in enumerate(layers):
            model.add_module(f'fc{i}', nn.Linear(prev_size, layer['size']))
            model.add_module(f'act{i}', nn.ReLU())
            prev_size = layer['size']

        model.add_module('output', nn.Linear(prev_size, self.output_dim))

        return model

    def quantize(self, bits=8):
        """模型量化"""
        self.model = torch.quantization.quantize_dynamic(
            self.model,
            {nn.Linear},
            dtype=torch.qint8
        )

    def prune(self, sparsity=0.5):
        """模型剪枝"""
        for module in self.model.modules():
            if isinstance(module, nn.Linear):
                # 基于权重大小剪枝
                weight = module.weight.data
                threshold = torch.quantile(torch.abs(weight), sparsity)
                mask = torch.abs(weight) > threshold
                module.weight.data *= mask
```

**联邦边缘学习**：

```python
class EdgeFederatedLearning:
    def __init__(self, edge_nodes, cloud_server):
        self.edge_nodes = edge_nodes
        self.cloud = cloud_server
        self.sync_interval = 3600  # 每小时同步

    def local_edge_training(self, edge_node):
        """边缘节点本地训练"""
        # 使用本地数据训练
        edge_node.local_train()

        # 如果满足条件，上传到云
        if edge_node.should_upload():
            update = edge_node.get_model_update()
            self.cloud.receive_update(edge_node.id, update)

    def cloud_aggregation(self):
        """云端聚合"""
        updates = self.cloud.get_all_updates()

        # 聚合更新
        aggregated = self.aggregate(updates)

        # 分发全局模型
        for edge_node in self.edge_nodes:
            edge_node.receive_global_model(aggregated)

    def adaptive_sync(self):
        """自适应同步策略"""
        for edge_node in self.edge_nodes:
            # 根据网络状况调整同步频率
            network_quality = edge_node.measure_network()

            if network_quality > 0.8:
                edge_node.sync_interval = 1800  # 网络好，频繁同步
            elif network_quality > 0.5:
                edge_node.sync_interval = 3600  # 网络一般，正常同步
            else:
                edge_node.sync_interval = 7200  # 网络差，减少同步
```

### 61.2 传感器融合

**多模态融合**：

```python
class MultiModalFusion:
    def __init__(self, modalities):
        self.modalities = modalities
        self.encoders = {}
        self.fusion_layer = None

        for modality in modalities:
            self.encoders[modality] = self.build_encoder(modality)

        self.fusion_layer = self.build_fusion_layer()

    def build_encoder(self, modality):
        """为每种模态构建编码器"""
        if modality == 'visual':
            return CNNEncoder()
        elif modality == 'audio':
            return AudioEncoder()
        elif modality == 'text':
            return TextEncoder()
        elif modality == 'sensor':
            return SensorEncoder()

    def build_fusion_layer(self):
        """构建融合层"""
        return nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(len(self.modalities) * 128, 256),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Linear(256, 128)
        )

    def early_fusion(self, inputs):
        """早期融合：在特征层面融合"""
        features = []
        for modality in self.modalities:
            encoded = self.encoders[modality](inputs[modality])
            features.append(encoded)

        # 拼接特征
        fused = torch.cat(features, dim=-1)
        output = self.fusion_layer(fused)

        return output

    def late_fusion(self, inputs):
        """晚期融合：在决策层面融合"""
        predictions = []
        for modality in self.modalities:
            encoded = self.encoders[modality](inputs[modality])
            pred = self.decision_layer(encoded)
            predictions.append(pred)

        # 加权平均
        weights = self.learn_weights(predictions)
        fused = sum(w * p for w, p in zip(weights, predictions))

        return fused

    def attention_fusion(self, inputs):
        """注意力融合"""
        features = []
        for modality in self.modalities:
            encoded = self.encoders[modality](inputs[modality])
            features.append(encoded)

        # 计算注意力权重
        attention_weights = self.attention_mechanism(features)

        # 加权融合
        fused = sum(w * f for w, f in zip(attention_weights, features))

        return fused
```

## 第六十二章：自动驾驶场景应用

### 62.1 感知与预测

**多目标跟踪**：

```python
class MultiObjectTracker:
    def __init__(self):
        self.tracks = {}
        self.next_id = 0
        self.max_age = 5
        self.min_hits = 3

    def update(self, detections):
        """更新跟踪器"""
        # 预测现有轨迹
        for track_id in self.tracks:
            self.tracks[track_id].predict()

        # 关联检测和轨迹
        matches, unmatched_detections, unmatched_tracks = self.associate(detections)

        # 更新匹配的轨迹
        for track_id, detection_id in matches:
            self.tracks[track_id].update(detections[detection_id])

        # 初始化新轨迹
        for detection_id in unmatched_detections:
            if len(detections[detection_id]) > self.min_hits:
                self.tracks[self.next_id] = Track(detections[detection_id])
                self.next_id += 1

        # 删除丢失的轨迹
        for track_id in unmatched_tracks:
            self.tracks[track_id].age += 1
            if self.tracks[track_id].age > self.max_age:
                del self.tracks[track_id]

        return self.tracks

    def associate(self, detections):
        """匈牙利算法关联"""
        # 计算代价矩阵
        cost_matrix = np.zeros((len(self.tracks), len(detections)))

        for i, (track_id, track) in enumerate(self.tracks.items()):
            for j, detection in enumerate(detections):
                cost_matrix[i, j] = self.compute_cost(track, detection)

        # 匈牙利算法
        row_indices, col_indices = linear_sum_assignment(cost_matrix)

        matches = []
        unmatched_detections = set(range(len(detections)))
        unmatched_tracks = set(self.tracks.keys())

        for row, col in zip(row_indices, col_indices):
            if cost_matrix[row, col] < self.gating_threshold:
                track_id = list(self.tracks.keys())[row]
                matches.append((track_id, col))
                unmatched_detections.discard(col)
                unmatched_tracks.discard(track_id)

        return matches, unmatched_detections, unmatched_tracks

class Track:
    def __init__(self, initial_detection):
        self.kalman_filter = KalmanFilter()
        self.kalman_filter.initiate(initial_detection)
        self.hits = 1
        self.age = 0
        self.history = [initial_detection]

    def predict(self):
        self.kalman_filter.predict()

    def update(self, detection):
        self.kalman_filter.update(detection)
        self.hits += 1
        self.age = 0
        self.history.append(detection)
```

**轨迹预测**：

```python
class TrajectoryPredictor:
    def __init__(self, encoder, decoder):
        self.encoder = encoder  # 编码历史轨迹
        self.decoder = decoder  # 解码未来轨迹

    def predict(self, trajectory_history, scene_context, num_modes=5):
        """多模态轨迹预测"""
        # 编码历史
        history_encoding = self.encoder(trajectory_history)

        # 结合场景上下文
        scene_encoding = self.encode_scene(scene_context)
        combined = torch.cat([history_encoding, scene_encoding], dim=-1)

        # 生成多模态预测
        predictions = []
        probabilities = []

        for mode in range(num_modes):
            # 每个模态一个可能的未来
            future_trajectory = self.decoder(combined, mode)
            prob = self.compute_mode_probability(combined, mode)

            predictions.append(future_trajectory)
            probabilities.append(prob)

        # 归一化概率
        probabilities = F.softmax(torch.stack(probabilities), dim=0)

        return predictions, probabilities

    def compute_mode_probability(self, encoding, mode):
        """计算每个模态的概率"""
        return self.mode_classifier(encoding, mode)
```

### 62.2 决策规划

**行为规划**：

```python
class BehaviorPlanner:
    def __init__(self):
        self.behaviors = ['KEEP_LANE', 'CHANGE_LEFT', 'CHANGE_RIGHT', 'FOLLOW', 'STOP']
        self.policy = BehaviorPolicyNetwork()

    def plan(self, ego_state, surrounding_tracks, map_info):
        """行为层规划"""
        # 构建场景表示
        scene = self.build_scene(ego_state, surrounding_tracks, map_info)

        # 评估每种行为
        behavior_scores = {}
        for behavior in self.behaviors:
            if self.is_feasible(behavior, scene):
                score = self.evaluate_behavior(behavior, scene)
                behavior_scores[behavior] = score

        # 选择最优行为
        best_behavior = max(behavior_scores, key=behavior_scores.get)

        return best_behavior

    def evaluate_behavior(self, behavior, scene):
        """评估行为的安全性和效率"""
        safety_score = self.evaluate_safety(behavior, scene)
        efficiency_score = self.evaluate_efficiency(behavior, scene)
        comfort_score = self.evaluate_comfort(behavior, scene)

        return 0.5 * safety_score + 0.3 * efficiency_score + 0.2 * comfort_score
```

**运动规划**：

```python
class MotionPlanner:
    def __init__(self):
        self.constraints = Constraints()
        self.cost_function = CostFunction()

    def plan_trajectory(self, start_state, goal_state, obstacles, behavior):
        """规划轨迹"""
        if behavior == 'FOLLOW':
            return self.plan_following_trajectory(start_state, goal_state, obstacles)
        elif behavior == 'CHANGE_LEFT':
            return self.plan_lane_change(start_state, goal_state, obstacles, 'left')
        elif behavior == 'STOP':
            return self.plan_stop_trajectory(start_state, obstacles)

    def plan_following_trajectory(self, start_state, goal_state, obstacles):
        """跟车轨迹规划"""
        # 使用Frenet坐标系
        frenet_path = self.to_frenet(start_state)

        # 采样候选轨迹
        candidates = self.sample_trajectories(frenet_path)

        # 评估并选择最优轨迹
        best_trajectory = None
        best_cost = float('inf')

        for trajectory in candidates:
            if self.is_collision_free(trajectory, obstacles):
                cost = self.cost_function.evaluate(trajectory)
                if cost < best_cost:
                    best_cost = cost
                    best_trajectory = trajectory

        return self.to_cartesian(best_trajectory)

    def sample_trajectories(self, frenet_path):
        """采样候选轨迹"""
        candidates = []

        # 纵向采样（速度-时间）
        for target_speed in np.linspace(0, self.max_speed, 10):
            for time_to_target in np.linspace(2, 10, 10):
                longitudinal = self.plan_longitudinal(frenet_path.s, target_speed, time_to_target)

                # 横向采样
                for lateral_offset in np.linspace(-3, 3, 10):
                    lateral = self.plan_lateral(frenet_path.d, lateral_offset, time_to_target)

                    trajectory = self.combine_longitudinal_lateral(longitudinal, lateral)
                    candidates.append(trajectory)

        return candidates
```

## 第六十三章：金融交易应用

### 63.1 市场预测

**多因子模型**：

```python
class MultiFactorModel:
    def __init__(self, factors):
        self.factors = factors
        self.factor_weights = None
        self.factor_returns = None

    def calculate_factor_exposures(self, assets):
        """计算资产的因子暴露"""
        exposures = {}

        for asset in assets:
            asset_exposures = {}
            for factor in self.factors:
                exposure = factor.calculate_exposure(asset)
                asset_exposures[factor.name] = exposure
            exposures[asset] = asset_exposures

        return exposures

    def estimate_factor_returns(self, returns, exposures):
        """估计因子收益（横截面回归）"""
        # 对每个时间点进行回归
        factor_returns = []

        for t in range(len(returns)):
            X = np.array([exposures[asset] for asset in returns.columns])
            y = returns.iloc[t].values

            # 加权最小二乘
            weights = self.calculate_weights(returns.iloc[t])
            W = np.diag(weights)

            beta = np.linalg.lstsq(X.T @ W @ X, X.T @ W @ y, rcond=None)[0]
            factor_returns.append(beta)

        self.factor_returns = pd.DataFrame(factor_returns, columns=self.factors)
        return self.factor_returns

    def predict_returns(self, exposures):
        """预测资产收益"""
        if self.factor_returns is None:
            raise ValueError("Factor returns not estimated")

        # 使用最新因子收益预测
        latest_factor_returns = self.factor_returns.iloc[-1]

        predictions = {}
        for asset, asset_exposures in exposures.items():
            predicted_return = sum(
                asset_exposures[factor] * latest_factor_returns[factor]
                for factor in self.factors
            )
            predictions[asset] = predicted_return

        return predictions
```

**高频交易信号**：

```python
class HighFrequencySignal:
    def __init__(self, order_book, trade_history):
        self.order_book = order_book
        self.trade_history = trade_history

    def calculate_order_imbalance(self):
        """计算订单簿不平衡"""
        bid_volume = sum(level['volume'] for level in self.order_book.bids[:5])
        ask_volume = sum(level['volume'] for level in self.order_book.asks[:5])

        imbalance = (bid_volume - ask_volume) / (bid_volume + ask_volume)
        return imbalance

    def calculate_flow_toxicity(self):
        """计算VPIN（Volume-Synchronized Probability of Informed Trading）"""
        # 计算买卖压力
        buy_volume = sum(t['volume'] for t in self.trade_history if t['side'] == 'buy')
        sell_volume = sum(t['volume'] for t in self.trade_history if t['side'] == 'sell')

        # 计算VPIN
        total_volume = buy_volume + sell_volume
        if total_volume == 0:
            return 0

        vpin = abs(buy_volume - sell_volume) / total_volume
        return vpin

    def generate_signal(self):
        """生成交易信号"""
        imbalance = self.calculate_order_imbalance()
        vpin = self.calculate_flow_toxicity()

        # 综合信号
        if imbalance > 0.3 and vpin < 0.3:
            return 'BUY'
        elif imbalance < -0.3 and vpin < 0.3:
            return 'SELL'
        elif vpin > 0.7:
            return 'AVOID'  # 有毒流，避免交易
        else:
            return 'NEUTRAL'
```

### 63.2 风险管理

**风险价值（VaR）计算**：

```python
class RiskManager:
    def __init__(self, portfolio, confidence_level=0.95):
        self.portfolio = portfolio
        self.confidence_level = confidence_level

    def calculate_var(self, method='historical', horizon=1):
        """计算风险价值"""
        if method == 'historical':
            return self.historical_var(horizon)
        elif method == 'parametric':
            return self.parametric_var(horizon)
        elif method == 'monte_carlo':
            return self.monte_carlo_var(horizon)

    def historical_var(self, horizon):
        """历史模拟法"""
        returns = self.portfolio.get_historical_returns()

        # 计算组合收益
        portfolio_returns = self.calculate_portfolio_returns(returns)

        # 计算VaR
        var = np.percentile(portfolio_returns, (1 - self.confidence_level) * 100)

        return abs(var) * np.sqrt(horizon)

    def parametric_var(self, horizon):
        """参数法（方差-协方差法）"""
        returns = self.portfolio.get_historical_returns()

        # 计算均值和协方差
        mean = returns.mean()
        cov = returns.cov()

        # 组合权重
        weights = self.portfolio.get_weights()

        # 组合均值和方差
        portfolio_mean = weights.T @ mean
        portfolio_var = weights.T @ cov @ weights

        # 正态分布分位数
        z_score = stats.norm.ppf(1 - self.confidence_level)

        var = -(portfolio_mean + z_score * np.sqrt(portfolio_var))

        return var * np.sqrt(horizon)

    def monte_carlo_var(self, horizon, n_simulations=10000):
        """蒙特卡洛模拟法"""
        returns = self.portfolio.get_historical_returns()

        # 估计参数
        mean = returns.mean().values
        cov = returns.cov().values

        # 生成随机样本
        simulated_returns = np.random.multivariate_normal(mean, cov, n_simulations)

        # 计算组合收益
        weights = self.portfolio.get_weights().values
        portfolio_returns = simulated_returns @ weights

        # 计算VaR
        var = np.percentile(portfolio_returns, (1 - self.confidence_level) * 100)

        return abs(var) * np.sqrt(horizon)
```

## 第六十四章：医疗诊断应用

### 64.1 医学影像分析

**病灶检测**：

```python
class LesionDetector:
    def __init__(self, backbone='resnet50'):
        self.backbone = self.build_backbone(backbone)
        self.rpn = RegionProposalNetwork()
        self.roi_head = ROIHead()

    def detect(self, image):
        """检测病灶"""
        # 特征提取
        features = self.backbone(image)

        # 生成候选区域
        proposals, scores = self.rpn(features)

        # 分类和回归
        detections = self.roi_head(features, proposals)

        # 非极大值抑制
        final_detections = self.nms(detections)

        return final_detections

    def nms(self, detections, threshold=0.5):
        """非极大值抑制"""
        boxes = detections['boxes']
        scores = detections['scores']

        # 按分数排序
        indices = scores.argsort(descending=True)

        keep = []
        while len(indices) > 0:
            current = indices[0]
            keep.append(current)

            if len(indices) == 1:
                break

            # 计算IoU
            ious = self.compute_iou(boxes[current], boxes[indices[1:]])

            # 保留IoU小于阈值的框
            mask = ious < threshold
            indices = indices[1:][mask]

        return {k: v[keep] for k, v in detections.items()}
```

**报告生成**：

```python
class MedicalReportGenerator:
    def __init__(self, encoder, decoder):
        self.encoder = encoder  # 图像编码器
        self.decoder = decoder  # 文本解码器

    def generate(self, image, findings):
        """生成医学报告"""
        # 编码图像
        image_features = self.encoder(image)

        # 编码发现
        findings_features = self.encode_findings(findings)

        # 生成报告
        context = torch.cat([image_features, findings_features], dim=-1)

        report = []
        for _ in range(self.max_length):
            # 生成下一个词
            logits = self.decoder(context, report)
            word = self.sample_word(logits)

            if word == '<END>':
                break

            report.append(word)

        return ' '.join(report)

    def encode_findings(self, findings):
        """编码临床发现"""
        embeddings = []
        for finding in findings:
            embedding = self.finding_encoder(finding)
            embeddings.append(embedding)

        return torch.mean(torch.stack(embeddings), dim=0)
```

### 64.2 药物发现

**分子生成**：

```python
class MoleculeGenerator:
    def __init__(self, vocab, encoder, decoder):
        self.vocab = vocab  # SMILES词汇表
        self.encoder = encoder
        self.decoder = decoder

    def generate(self, properties, num_samples=100):
        """生成满足性质的分子"""
        molecules = []

        for _ in range(num_samples):
            # 从性质生成潜在表示
            z = self.sample_latent(properties)

            # 解码为SMILES
            smiles = self.decode(z)

            # 验证分子
            if self.is_valid(smiles):
                molecules.append(smiles)

        return molecules

    def sample_latent(self, properties):
        """从条件分布采样潜在变量"""
        mean, logvar = self.encoder(properties)
        z = self.reparameterize(mean, logvar)
        return z

    def decode(self, z):
        """解码潜在变量为SMILES"""
        smiles = []
        current_token = '<START>'

        for _ in range(self.max_length):
            logits = self.decoder(z, current_token)
            current_token = self.sample_token(logits)

            if current_token == '<END>':
                break

            smiles.append(current_token)

        return ''.join(smiles)

    def is_valid(self, smiles):
        """验证SMILES是否有效"""
        try:
            mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles)
            return mol is not None
        except:
            return False
```

## 第六十五章：自然语言处理应用

### 65.1 对话系统

**多轮对话管理**：

```python
class DialogueManager:
    def __init__(self, nlu, dst, policy, nlg):
        self.nlu = nlu  # 自然语言理解
        self.dst = dst  # 对话状态跟踪
        self.policy = policy  # 对话策略
        self.nlg = nlg  # 自然语言生成
        self.dialogue_history = []

    def process_turn(self, user_utterance):
        """处理一轮对话"""
        # 1. 自然语言理解
        intent, slots = self.nlu.parse(user_utterance)

        # 2. 更新对话状态
        self.dst.update(intent, slots)
        dialogue_state = self.dst.get_state()

        # 3. 选择系统动作
        system_action = self.policy.select_action(dialogue_state)

        # 4. 自然语言生成
        response = self.nlg.generate(system_action)

        # 5. 更新历史
        self.dialogue_history.append({
            'user': user_utterance,
            'intent': intent,
            'slots': slots,
            'state': dialogue_state,
            'action': system_action,
            'response': response
        })

        return response

    def reset(self):
        """重置对话"""
        self.dst.reset()
        self.dialogue_history = []
```

class EmotionalDialogue:
    def __init__(self, base_model, emotion_classifier):
        self.base_model = base_model
        self.emotion_classifier = emotion_classifier
        self.current_emotion = 'neutral'

    def generate_response(self, user_input, target_emotion=None):
        """生成带情感的回复"""
        # 识别用户情感
        user_emotion = self.emotion_classifier.classify(user_input)

        # 确定目标情感
        if target_emotion is None:
            target_emotion = self.select_emotion(user_emotion)

        # 生成基础回复
        base_response = self.base_model.generate(user_input)

        # 调整情感
        emotional_response = self.adjust_emotion(base_response, target_emotion)

        self.current_emotion = target_emotion

        return emotional_response

    def select_emotion(self, user_emotion):
        """选择适当的情感回应"""
        # 共情策略
        empathy_map = {
            'happy': 'happy',
            'sad': 'sympathetic',
            'angry': 'calm',
            'fearful': 'reassuring'
        }

        return empathy_map.get(user_emotion, 'neutral')

    def adjust_emotion(self, text, target_emotion):
        """调整文本情感"""
        # 使用情感风格迁移
        return self.style_transfer(text, target_emotion)

### 65.2 知识图谱

**知识图谱嵌入**：

```python
class KnowledgeGraphEmbedding:
    def __init__(self, num_entities, num_relations, embedding_dim):
        self.entity_embeddings = nn.Embedding(num_entities, embedding_dim)
        self.relation_embeddings = nn.Embedding(num_relations, embedding_dim)

    def trans_e_score(self, head, relation, tail):
        """TransE评分函数"""
        h = self.entity_embeddings(head)
        r = self.relation_embeddings(relation)
        t = self.entity_embeddings(tail)

        score = torch.norm(h + r - t, p=1, dim=-1)
        return score

    def trans_r_score(self, head, relation, tail):
        """TransR评分函数"""
        h = self.entity_embeddings(head)
        r = self.relation_embeddings(relation)
        t = self.entity_embeddings(tail)

        # 投影矩阵
        projection = self.projection_matrices[relation]
        h_proj = torch.matmul(h, projection)
        t_proj = torch.matmul(t, projection)

        score = torch.norm(h_proj + r - t_proj, p=1, dim=-1)
        return score

    def train(self, triplets, negative_triplets):
        """训练知识图谱嵌入"""
        optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.parameters(), lr=0.001)

        for epoch in range(1000):
            # 正样本
            pos_score = self.trans_e_score(triplets[:, 0], triplets[:, 1], triplets[:, 2])

            # 负样本
            neg_score = self.trans_e_score(negative_triplets[:, 0], negative_triplets[:, 1], negative_triplets[:, 2])

            # 间隔损失
            loss = torch.mean(F.relu(1.0 + pos_score - neg_score))

            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
```
